# Entire Wingman - Implementation Plan

## Context

The wingman feature adds an automated code review loop to the CLI. After an agent turn that modifies files, a background reviewer analyzes the changes (using checkpoint data to understand intent), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and then — after a short delay — triggers the main agent to apply those suggestions via `claude --continue -p`. This creates a self-improving development loop where agent output gets automatically reviewed and refined.

## Architecture Overview

```
Agent turn ends (stop hook)
    │
    ├── SaveChanges (existing)
    ├── transitionSessionTurnEnd (existing)
    └── [NEW] If wingman enabled && files changed && no pending REVIEW.md && not already reviewed:
        │
        └── Spawn detached: `entire wingman __review <json>`
            │
            ├── Sleep ~10s (let agent turn fully settle)
            ├── git diff HEAD (get code changes)
            ├── Read user prompts from payload (for intent)
            ├── claude --print --model sonnet (review)
            ├── Write .entire/REVIEW.md
            └── Wait ~30s, then if session still IDLE:
                └── claude --continue -p "Apply .entire/REVIEW.md" --setting-sources ""
                    │
                    └── Agent applies suggestions, deletes REVIEW.md
                        (no hooks fire due to --setting-sources "",
                         so no infinite loop)
```

## Implementation Steps

### 1. Settings support

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`**

Add `IsWingmanEnabled()` method + package-level convenience function, following the `IsSummarizeEnabled()` pattern. Settings path: `strategy_options.wingman.enabled`.

### 2. `entire wingman` command group

**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`**

Cobra command group with subcommands:
- `entire wingman enable` — Sets `strategy_options.wingman.enabled = true` in settings. Validates Entire is enabled first.
- `entire wingman disable` — Sets it to `false`. Deletes `.entire/REVIEW.md` if present.
- `entire wingman status` — Shows enabled state + last review timestamp from `.entire/wingman-state.json`.

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/root.go`** — Register `newWingmanCmd()`.

### 3. Hidden `__review` command (detached worker)

**Within `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`** (or separate `wingman_review.go`):

Hidden command: `entire wingman __review <json-payload>`

Steps:
1. Unmarshal `WingmanPayload` (session ID, repo root, modified/new/deleted files, user prompts, commit message)
2. `cd` to repo root
3. **Initial delay**: Sleep ~10s to let the agent turn fully settle
4. Compute diff: `git diff HEAD` for uncommitted changes, or `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` if auto-commit strategy just committed
5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + file list)
6. Call `claude --print --model sonnet --output-format json --setting-sources ""` with prompt via stdin (reuse `stripGitEnv` pattern from `summarize/claude.go`, run from `os.TempDir()`)
7. Parse response, extract review text
8. Write `.entire/REVIEW.md`
9. Update `.entire/wingman-state.json` (dedup tracking)
10. **Auto-apply phase**: Sleep ~30s, then:
   - Load session state, check phase is `IDLE`
   - If idle: spawn `claude --continue -p "Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md." --setting-sources ""`
   - `--setting-sources ""` disables all hooks on the automated process — prevents recursive wingman triggers and session state conflicts
   - If not idle (user is active): exit, leave REVIEW.md for notification

### 4. Detached subprocess spawner

**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go`** (`//go:build unix`)
**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go`** (`//go:build !unix`)

Follow exact pattern from `telemetry/detached_unix.go`:
- `os.Executable()` to get own binary
- `Setpgid: true` for process group detachment
- `cmd.Start()` + `Process.Release()`
- Non-unix stub is a no-op (log warning)

### 5. Hook integration (stop hook trigger)

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**

After `transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)` in `commitWithMetadata()` (~line 378):

```go
if totalChanges > 0 && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    // triggerWingmanReview checks for pending REVIEW.md and dedup internally
    triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{
        SessionID:     sessionID,
        RepoRoot:      repoRoot,
        ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,
        NewFiles:      relNewFiles,
        DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,
        Prompts:       allPrompts,
        CommitMessage: commitMessage,
    })
}
```

`triggerWingmanReview` (in wingman.go):
1. Check if `.entire/REVIEW.md` already exists — skip if a pending review hasn't been applied yet
2. Check deduplication: compute SHA256 of sorted file paths, compare against `.entire/wingman-state.json`. Skip if same hash (already reviewed these exact changes).
3. Marshal payload to JSON
4. Call `spawnDetachedWingmanReview(jsonStr)`
5. Print to stderr: `"[wingman] Review starting in background...\n"`

### 6. Notification on next prompt (fallback)

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**

In `captureInitialState()`, after existing logic:

```go
if settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {
    if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "REVIEW.md")); err == nil {
        fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md — review and apply suggestions\n")
    }
}
```

### 7. Review prompt template

**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go`**

Prompt instructs Claude to act as a senior code reviewer. Key elements:
- User's intent (from prompts/commit message)
- Full code diff
- File change list
- Checkpoint context for understanding "why"

Output format: structured Markdown with severity levels (`CRITICAL` / `WARNING` / `SUGGESTION`), file paths, line numbers, descriptions, and fix suggestions.

### 8. Deduplication & state tracking

**State file: `.entire/wingman-state.json`**

```json
{
  "session_id": "...",
  "files_hash": "sha256-of-sorted-file-paths",
  "reviewed_at": "2026-02-11T...",
  "review_applied": false
}
```

- Before spawning review: skip if `files_hash` matches current
- After writing REVIEW.md: update state
- `.entire/wingman-state.json` is already inside `.entire/` (gitignored)

### 9. Loop-breaking mechanism

The `--setting-sources ""` flag on the auto-apply `claude --continue` call disables all Claude Code hooks (including Entire's stop hook). This means:
- No `commitWithMetadata()` fires after the auto-apply turn
- No wingman trigger, no infinite loop
- The file edits are still in the working directory
- The next human-initiated turn will checkpoint them normally

### 10. Tests

**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go`**
- Settings parsing (`IsWingmanEnabled` with various configs)
- Dedup logic (files hash computation, skip-if-same-hash)
- Review prompt building (correct template interpolation, diff truncation)
- Payload serialization/deserialization round-trip
- `triggerWingmanReview` skips when disabled / when dedup matches

**File: `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings_test.go`** (if exists)
- `IsWingmanEnabled()` with strategy_options variations

| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go` | Review prompt template |
| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached process spawner (unix) |

## Existing code to reuse

- `summarize/claude.go` — `stripGitEnv()`, Claude CLI invocation pattern, JSON response parsing
- `telemetry/detached_unix.go` — detached subprocess spawn pattern
- `settings/settings.go` — `IsSummarizeEnabled()` pattern for feature flags
- `session/phase.go` — Phase check (IDLE detection for auto-apply timing)
- `strategy.LoadSessionState()` — Read session phase from `.git/entire-sessions/`

## Edge cases

- **Claude CLI not installed**: Detached subprocess catches `exec.Error`, logs to `.entire/logs/`, exits silently
- **Large diffs**: Truncate diff at ~100KB in the prompt to keep review quality high
- **Race with user**: 30s delay + phase check prevents conflicts; if user starts typing, auto-apply is skipped
- **Multiple concurrent sessions**: Each session has its own session ID in the payload; dedup is per-session

## Verification

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` — all pass
2. Manual test: enable wingman, make a change with Claude, verify:
   - `.entire/REVIEW.md` appears after ~10s
   - Review contains structured feedback referencing the actual diff
   - After ~30s (if idle), Claude auto-resumes and applies suggestions
   - REVIEW.md is deleted after application
   - No infinite loop (next stop hook does not re-trigger wingman for same files)
3. Test fallback: start typing before 30s elapses, verify REVIEW.md notification appears on next prompt-submit

     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// DefaultStrategyName is the default strategy when none is configured.
    18→// This is duplicated here to avoid importing the strategy package (which would create a cycle).
    19→const DefaultStrategyName = "manual-commit"
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    23→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    24→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    25→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    29→type EntireSettings struct {
    30→	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
    31→	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
    32→
    33→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    34→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    35→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    36→
    37→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    38→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    39→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    42→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    43→	// Defaults to "info".
    44→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    45→
    46→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    47→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    48→
    49→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    50→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    51→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    55→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    56→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    57→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    58→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    59→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    60→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    63→	}
    64→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Load base settings
    70→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    76→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    80→		}
    81→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    82→	} else {
    83→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    84→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	applyDefaults(settings)
    89→
    90→	return settings, nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    94→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    95→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    96→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    97→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
    98→}
    99→
   100→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
   101→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
   102→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   103→	settings := &EntireSettings{
   104→		Strategy: DefaultStrategyName,
   105→		Enabled:  true, // Default to enabled
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   111→			return settings, nil
   112→		}
   113→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   117→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   118→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→	applyDefaults(settings)
   122→
   123→	return settings, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   127→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   128→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   129→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   130→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   131→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   132→	var temp EntireSettings
   133→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   138→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   139→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Override strategy if present and non-empty
   144→	if strategyRaw, ok := raw["strategy"]; ok {
   145→		var s string
   146→		if err := json.Unmarshal(strategyRaw, &s); err != nil {
   147→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy field: %w", err)
   148→		}
   149→		if s != "" {
   150→			settings.Strategy = s
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Override enabled if present
   155→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   156→		var e bool
   157→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   158→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   159→		}
   160→		settings.Enabled = e
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Override local_dev if present
   164→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   165→		var ld bool
   166→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   167→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   168→		}
   169→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   173→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   174→		var ll string
   175→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   176→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   177→		}
   178→		if ll != "" {
   179→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   180→		}
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   184→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   185→		var opts map[string]any
   186→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   187→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   188→		}
   189→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   190→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   191→		} else {
   192→			for k, v := range opts {
   193→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   194→			}
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Override telemetry if present
   199→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   200→		var t bool
   201→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   202→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   203→		}
   204→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	return nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func applyDefaults(settings *EntireSettings) {
   211→	if settings.Strategy == "" {
   212→		settings.Strategy = DefaultStrategyName
   213→	}
   214→}
   215→
   216→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   217→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   218→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   219→	settings, err := Load()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return false
   222→	}
   223→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   224→}
   225→
   226→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   227→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   228→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   229→		return false
   230→	}
   231→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   232→	if !ok {
   233→		return false
   234→	}
   235→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   236→	if !ok {
   237→		return false
   238→	}
   239→	return enabled
   240→}
   241→
   242→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   243→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   244→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   245→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   246→		return false
   247→	}
   248→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   249→	if !exists {
   250→		return false
   251→	}
   252→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   253→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   254→	}
   255→	return false
   256→}
   257→
   258→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   259→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   260→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   261→}
   262→
   263→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   264→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   265→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   266→}
   267→
   268→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   269→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   270→	// Get absolute path for the file
   271→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Ensure directory exists
   277→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   278→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   288→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→	return nil
   292→}
   293→

    28→
    29→func NewRootCmd() *cobra.Command {
    30→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    31→		Use:   "entire",
    32→		Short: "Entire CLI",
    33→		Long:  "The command-line interface for Entire" + gettingStarted + accessibilityHelp,
    34→		// Let main.go handle error printing to avoid duplication
    35→		SilenceErrors: true,
    36→		SilenceUsage:  true,
    37→		// Hide completion command from help but keep it functional
    38→		CompletionOptions: cobra.CompletionOptions{
    39→			HiddenDefaultCmd: true,
    40→		},
    41→		PersistentPostRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) {
    42→			// Skip for hidden commands (walk parent chain — Cobra doesn't propagate Hidden)
    43→			for c := cmd; c != nil; c = c.Parent() {
    44→				if c.Hidden {
    45→					return
    46→				}
    47→			}
    48→
    49→			// Load settings once for telemetry and version check
    50→			var telemetryEnabled *bool
    51→			settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    52→			if err == nil {
    53→				telemetryEnabled = settings.Telemetry
    54→			}
    55→
    56→			// Check if telemetry is enabled
    57→			if telemetryEnabled != nil && *telemetryEnabled {
    58→				// Use detached tracking (non-blocking)
    59→				installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
    60→				agentStr := JoinAgentNames(installedAgents)
    61→				telemetry.TrackCommandDetached(cmd, settings.Strategy, agentStr, settings.Enabled, buildinfo.Version)
    62→			}
    63→
    64→			// Version check and notification (synchronous with 2s timeout)
    65→			// Runs AFTER command completes to avoid interfering with interactive modes
    66→			versioncheck.CheckAndNotify(cmd.OutOrStdout(), buildinfo.Version)
    67→		},
    68→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    69→			return cmd.Help()
    70→		},

    95→func newVersionCmd() *cobra.Command {

   102→			fmt.Printf("OS/Arch: %s/%s\n", runtime.GOOS, runtime.GOARCH)

   105→}

   107→// newSendAnalyticsCmd creates the hidden command for sending analytics from a detached subprocess.
   108→// This command is invoked by TrackCommandDetached and should not be called directly by users.
   109→func newSendAnalyticsCmd() *cobra.Command {
   110→	return &cobra.Command{
   111→		Use:    "__send_analytics",
   112→		Hidden: true,
   113→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
   114→		Run: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) {
   115→			telemetry.SendEvent(args[0])
   116→		},
   117→	}
   118→}

     1→// hooks_claudecode_handlers.go contains Claude Code specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (

     9→	"log/slog"

    22→
    23→// hookInputData contains parsed hook input and session identifiers.
    24→type hookInputData struct {
    25→	agent     agent.Agent
    26→	input     *agent.HookInput
    27→	sessionID string
    28→}
    29→
    30→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    31→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    32→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    39→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    45→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    46→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    47→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    48→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    49→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    50→	)
    51→
    52→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    53→	if sessionID == "" {
    54→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    58→	return &hookInputData{
    59→		agent:     ag,
    60→		input:     input,
    61→		sessionID: sessionID,
    62→	}, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    66→func captureInitialState() error {
    67→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    68→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    74→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    75→		return err
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    81→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    82→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    83→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	return nil
    88→}
    89→
    90→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    91→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    92→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
    93→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    99→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   105→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   106→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   107→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   108→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   109→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   110→	)
   111→
   112→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   113→	if sessionID == "" {
   114→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   118→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   119→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   123→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   124→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   125→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   128→	}
   129→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   130→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   134→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   135→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   136→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   137→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   138→
   139→	// Copy transcript
   140→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   141→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   145→
   146→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   147→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   148→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   154→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   155→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   156→	var transcriptOffset int
   157→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   158→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   159→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   160→	} else {
   161→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   162→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   163→		if loadErr != nil {
   164→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   165→		}
   166→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   167→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   168→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   173→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   174→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   175→	var totalLines int
   176→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   177→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   178→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   179→		if err != nil {
   180→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   181→		}
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   183→	} else {
   184→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   185→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   186→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   187→		if err != nil {
   188→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   189→		}
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   193→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   194→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   195→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   196→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   197→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   198→	}
   199→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   200→
   201→	// Extract summary
   202→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   203→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Get modified files from transcript
   210→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   211→
   212→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   213→	lastPrompt := ""
   214→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   215→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   216→	}
   217→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   218→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   219→
   220→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   221→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   222→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   223→	if err != nil {
   224→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	if preState != nil {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   232→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   238→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   239→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   240→	if changes != nil {
   241→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   242→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   246→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   247→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   248→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   249→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   250→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   251→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   252→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   253→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   254→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   255→		}
   256→		return nil
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   260→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   261→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   262→	}
   263→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   265→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   266→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   267→		}
   268→	}
   269→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   270→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   271→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   272→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Create context file before saving changes
   277→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   278→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   282→
   283→	// Get git author from local/global config
   284→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Get the configured strategy
   290→	strat := GetStrategy()
   291→
   292→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   293→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   294→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   298→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   299→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   300→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   304→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   305→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   306→	if preState != nil {
   307→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   308→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   312→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   313→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   314→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   315→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   316→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   319→		} else {
   320→			tokenUsage = usage
   321→		}
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   325→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   326→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   327→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   328→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   329→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   330→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   331→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   332→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   333→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   334→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   335→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   336→		AgentType:                agentType,
   337→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   338→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   339→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   343→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   347→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   348→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   349→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   350→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   351→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   352→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   353→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   354→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   355→		if loadErr != nil {
   356→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   357→		}
   358→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   359→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   360→		if sessionState == nil {
   361→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   362→				SessionID: sessionID,
   363→			}
   364→		}
   365→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   366→		sessionState.StepCount++
   367→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   368→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   369→		} else {
   370→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   371→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   376→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   377→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   378→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   379→
   380→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   381→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   382→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	return nil
   386→}
   387→
   388→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   389→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   390→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   391→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   392→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	// Get agent for logging context
   398→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   404→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   405→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   406→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   407→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   408→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   409→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   410→	)
   411→
   412→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   413→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   414→	if !found {
   415→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   416→		return nil
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   420→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   421→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   422→		return nil
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   426→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   429→		return nil
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   433→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   434→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   435→		return nil
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Get git author
   439→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   442→		return nil
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Get the active strategy
   446→	strat := GetStrategy()
   447→
   448→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   449→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   450→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   451→		return nil
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   455→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   456→	if sessionID == "" {
   457→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   461→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   462→
   463→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   464→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   465→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   466→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   467→	if todoContent == "" {
   468→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   469→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   470→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   471→		if todoCount > 0 {
   472→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   473→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   474→		}
   475→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   476→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   480→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   481→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   482→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   486→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   487→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   488→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   489→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   490→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   491→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   492→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   493→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   494→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   495→		IsIncremental:       true,
   496→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   497→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   498→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   499→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   500→		AgentType:           agentType,
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   504→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   505→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   506→		return nil
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   510→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   511→	return nil
   512→}
   513→
   514→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   515→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   516→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   517→	if err != nil {
   518→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Get agent for logging context
   522→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   528→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   529→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   530→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   531→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   532→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   533→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   534→	)
   535→
   536→	// Log context to stdout
   537→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   538→
   539→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   540→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   541→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   542→	// in file changes.
   543→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   544→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   545→	}
   546→
   547→	return nil
   548→}
   549→
   550→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   551→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   552→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   558→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   559→
   560→	// Get agent for logging context
   561→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Log parsed input context
   567→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   568→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   569→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   570→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   571→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   572→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   573→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   574→	)
   575→
   576→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   577→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   578→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   579→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   580→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   581→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   582→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   583→		}
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	// Log context to stdout
   587→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   588→
   589→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   590→	var modifiedFiles []string
   591→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   592→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   593→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   594→		if err != nil {
   595→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   596→		} else {
   597→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   598→		}
   599→	} else {
   600→		// Fall back to main transcript
   601→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   602→		if err != nil {
   603→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   604→		} else {
   605→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   606→		}
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   610→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   616→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   622→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   623→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   624→	if err != nil {
   625→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	// Filter and normalize paths
   629→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   630→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   631→	if changes != nil {
   632→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   633→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   634→	}
   635→
   636→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   637→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   638→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   639→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   640→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   641→		return nil
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   645→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   646→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   647→
   648→	// Get git author
   649→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   650→	if err != nil {
   651→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	// Get the configured strategy
   655→	strat := GetStrategy()
   656→
   657→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   658→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   659→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   663→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   664→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   665→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   666→	}
   667→
   668→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   669→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   670→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   671→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   672→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   673→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   674→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   675→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   676→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   677→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   678→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   679→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   680→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   681→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   682→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   683→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   684→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   685→		AgentType:              agentType,
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   689→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   690→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   694→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   695→
   696→	return nil
   697→}
   698→
   699→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   700→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   701→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   702→}
   703→
   704→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   705→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   706→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   707→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   708→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   709→	if err != nil {
   710→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   719→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   720→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   721→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   722→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   723→	)
   724→
   725→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   726→		return nil // No session to update
   727→	}
   728→
   729→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   730→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   731→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   732→	}
   733→	return nil
   734→}
   735→
   736→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   737→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   738→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   739→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   740→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   741→	if loadErr != nil {
   742→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   743→		return
   744→	}
   745→	if turnState == nil {
   746→		return
   747→	}
   748→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   749→
   750→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   751→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   752→		strat := GetStrategy()
   753→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   754→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   755→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   756→			}
   757→		}
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   761→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   762→	}
   763→}
   764→
   765→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   766→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   767→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   768→	if err != nil {
   769→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   770→	}
   771→	if state == nil {
   772→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   776→
   777→	now := time.Now()
   778→	state.EndedAt = &now
   779→
   780→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   781→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   782→	}
   783→	return nil
   784→}
   785→
   786→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   787→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   788→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   789→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   790→
   791→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   792→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   793→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   794→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   795→//
   796→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   797→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   798→//
   799→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   800→// with whatever data is available.
   801→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   802→	const (
   803→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   804→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   805→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   806→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   807→	)
   808→
   809→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   810→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   811→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   812→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   813→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   814→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   815→			)
   816→			return
   817→		}
   818→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   819→	}
   820→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   821→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   822→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   823→	)
   824→}
   825→
   826→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   827→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   828→// close to hookStartTime.
   829→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   830→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		return false
   833→	}
   834→	defer f.Close()
   835→
   836→	// Seek to tail
   837→	info, err := f.Stat()
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return false
   840→	}
   841→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   842→	if offset < 0 {
   843→		offset = 0
   844→	}
   845→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   846→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   847→		return false
   848→	}
   849→
   850→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   851→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   852→	for _, line := range lines {
   853→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   854→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   855→			continue
   856→		}
   857→
   858→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   859→		var entry struct {
   860→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   861→		}
   862→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   863→			continue
   864→		}
   865→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   866→		if err != nil {
   867→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   868→			if err != nil {
   869→				continue
   870→			}
   871→		}
   872→
   873→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   874→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   875→			return true
   876→		}
   877→	}
   878→	return false
   879→}
   880→

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package telemetry
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"syscall"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// spawnDetachedAnalytics spawns a detached subprocess to send analytics.
    14→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    15→// after the parent exits.
    16→func spawnDetachedAnalytics(payloadJSON string) {
    17→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    18→	if err != nil {
    19→		return
    20→	}
    21→
    22→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadJSON is controlled internally, not user input
    23→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "__send_analytics", payloadJSON)
    24→
    25→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    26→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    27→		Setpgid: true,
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Don't hold the working directory
    31→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    32→
    33→	// Inherit environment (may be needed for network config)
    34→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    35→
    36→	// Discard stdout/stderr to prevent output leaking to parent's terminal
    37→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    38→	cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    39→
    40→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    41→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    42→		return
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    46→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    47→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    48→}
    49→

     1→package summarize
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"os/exec"
    11→	"strings"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// summarizationPromptTemplate is the prompt used to generate summaries via the Claude CLI.
    17→//
    18→// Security note: The transcript is wrapped in <transcript> tags to provide clear boundary
    19→// markers. This helps contain any potentially malicious content within the transcript
    20→// (e.g., prompt injection attempts in user messages or file contents) by giving the LLM
    21→// a clear structural signal about where the untrusted content begins and ends.
    22→const summarizationPromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a structured summary.
    23→
    24→<transcript>
    25→%s
    26→</transcript>
    27→
    28→Return a JSON object with this exact structure:
    29→{
    30→  "intent": "What the user was trying to accomplish (1-2 sentences)",
    31→  "outcome": "What was actually achieved (1-2 sentences)",
    32→  "learnings": {
    33→    "repo": ["Codebase-specific patterns, conventions, or gotchas discovered"],
    34→    "code": [{"path": "file/path.go", "line": 42, "end_line": 56, "finding": "What was learned"}],
    35→    "workflow": ["General development practices or tool usage insights"]
    36→  },
    37→  "friction": ["Problems, blockers, or annoyances encountered"],
    38→  "open_items": ["Tech debt, unfinished work, or things to revisit later"]
    39→}
    40→
    41→Guidelines:
    42→- Be concise but specific
    43→- Include line numbers for code learnings when the transcript references specific lines
    44→- Friction should capture both blockers and minor annoyances
    45→- Open items are things intentionally deferred, not failures
    46→- Empty arrays are fine if a category doesn't apply
    47→- Return ONLY the JSON object, no markdown formatting or explanation`
    48→
    49→// DefaultModel is the default model used for summarization.
    50→// Sonnet provides a good balance of quality and cost, with 1M context window
    51→// to handle long transcripts without truncation.
    52→const DefaultModel = "sonnet"
    53→
    54→// ClaudeGenerator generates summaries using the Claude CLI.
    55→type ClaudeGenerator struct {
    56→	// ClaudePath is the path to the claude CLI executable.
    57→	// If empty, defaults to "claude" (expects it to be in PATH).
    58→	ClaudePath string
    59→
    60→	// Model is the Claude model to use for summarization.
    61→	// If empty, defaults to DefaultModel ("sonnet").
    62→	Model string
    63→
    64→	// CommandRunner allows injection of the command execution for testing.
    65→	// If nil, uses exec.CommandContext directly.
    66→	CommandRunner func(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) *exec.Cmd
    67→}
    68→
    69→// claudeCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI.
    70→type claudeCLIResponse struct {
    71→	Result string `json:"result"`
    72→}
    73→
    74→// Generate creates a summary from checkpoint data by calling the Claude CLI.
    75→func (g *ClaudeGenerator) Generate(ctx context.Context, input Input) (*checkpoint.Summary, error) {
    76→	// Format the transcript for the prompt
    77→	transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
    78→
    79→	// Build the prompt
    80→	prompt := buildSummarizationPrompt(transcriptText)
    81→
    82→	// Execute the Claude CLI
    83→	runner := g.CommandRunner
    84→	if runner == nil {
    85→		runner = exec.CommandContext
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	claudePath := g.ClaudePath
    89→	if claudePath == "" {
    90→		claudePath = "claude"
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	model := g.Model
    94→	if model == "" {
    95→		model = DefaultModel
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Use empty --setting-sources to skip all settings (user, project, local).
    99→	// This avoids loading MCP servers, hooks, or other config that could interfere
   100→	// with a simple --print summarization call.
   101→	cmd := runner(ctx, claudePath, "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", model, "--setting-sources", "")
   102→
   103→	// Fully isolate the subprocess from the user's git repo (ENT-242).
   104→	// Claude Code performs internal git operations (plugin cache, context gathering)
   105→	// that pollute the worktree index with phantom entries from its plugin cache.
   106→	// We must both change the working directory AND strip GIT_* env vars, because
   107→	// git hooks set GIT_DIR which lets Claude Code find the repo regardless of cwd.
   108→	// This also prevents recursive triggering of Entire's own git hooks.
   109→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   110→	cmd.Env = stripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   111→
   112→	// Pass prompt via stdin
   113→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   114→
   115→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   116→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   117→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   118→
   119→	err := cmd.Run()
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		// Check if the command was not found
   122→		var execErr *exec.Error
   123→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		// Check for exit error
   128→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   129→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   130→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Parse the CLI response
   137→	var cliResponse claudeCLIResponse
   138→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   139→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// The result field contains the actual JSON summary
   143→	resultJSON := cliResponse.Result
   144→
   145→	// Try to extract JSON if it's wrapped in markdown code blocks
   146→	resultJSON = extractJSONFromMarkdown(resultJSON)
   147→
   148→	// Parse the summary from the result
   149→	var summary checkpoint.Summary
   150→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(resultJSON), &summary); err != nil {
   151→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse summary JSON: %w (response: %s)", err, resultJSON)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	return &summary, nil
   155→}
   156→
   157→// buildSummarizationPrompt creates the prompt for the Claude CLI.
   158→func buildSummarizationPrompt(transcriptText string) string {
   159→	return fmt.Sprintf(summarizationPromptTemplate, transcriptText)
   160→}
   161→
   162→// stripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   163→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo.
   164→func stripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   165→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   166→	for _, e := range env {
   167→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   168→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→	return filtered
   172→}
   173→
   174→// extractJSONFromMarkdown attempts to extract JSON from markdown code blocks.
   175→// If the input is not wrapped in code blocks, it returns the input unchanged.
   176→func extractJSONFromMarkdown(s string) string {
   177→	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
   178→
   179→	// Check for ```json ... ``` blocks
   180→	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "```json") {
   181→		s = strings.TrimPrefix(s, "```json")
   182→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(s, "```"); idx != -1 {
   183→			s = s[:idx]
   184→		}
   185→		return strings.TrimSpace(s)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Check for ``` ... ``` blocks
   189→	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "```") {
   190→		s = strings.TrimPrefix(s, "```")
   191→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(s, "```"); idx != -1 {
   192→			s = s[:idx]
   193→		}
   194→		return strings.TrimSpace(s)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return s
   198→}
   199→

    18→// Package-level aliases to avoid shadowing the settings package with local variables named "settings".
    19→const (

    21→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = settings.EntireSettingsLocalFile

    23→
    24→// EntireSettings is an alias for settings.EntireSettings.
    25→type EntireSettings = settings.EntireSettings
    26→
    27→// LoadEntireSettings loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    28→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    29→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    30→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    31→func LoadEntireSettings() (*settings.EntireSettings, error) {
    32→	s, err := settings.Load()
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("loading settings: %w", err)
    35→	}
    36→	return s, nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// SaveEntireSettings saves the Entire settings to .entire/settings.json.
    40→func SaveEntireSettings(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
    41→	if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
    42→		return fmt.Errorf("saving settings: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→	return nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// SaveEntireSettingsLocal saves the Entire settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
    48→func SaveEntireSettingsLocal(s *settings.EntireSettings) error {
    49→	if err := settings.SaveLocal(s); err != nil {
    50→		return fmt.Errorf("saving local settings: %w", err)
    51→	}
    52→	return nil
    53→}
    54→
    55→// IsEnabled returns whether Entire is currently enabled.
    56→// Returns true by default if settings cannot be loaded.
    57→func IsEnabled() (bool, error) {
    58→	s, err := settings.Load()
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return true, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
    61→	}
    62→	return s.Enabled, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// GetStrategy returns the configured strategy instance.
    66→// Falls back to default if the configured strategy is not found.
    67→//
    68→//nolint:ireturn // Factory pattern requires returning the interface
    69→func GetStrategy() strategy.Strategy {
    70→	s, err := settings.Load()
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		// Fall back to default on error
    73→		logging.Info(context.Background(), "falling back to default strategy - failed to load settings",
    74→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
    75→		return strategy.Default()
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	strat, err := strategy.Get(s.Strategy)
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		// Fall back to default if strategy not found
    81→		logging.Info(context.Background(), "falling back to default strategy - configured strategy not found",
    82→			slog.String("configured", s.Strategy),
    83→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
    84→		return strategy.Default()
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	return strat
    88→}
    89→
    90→// GetLogLevel returns the configured log level from settings.
    91→// Returns empty string if not configured (caller should use default).
    92→// Note: ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL env var takes precedence; check it first.
    93→func GetLogLevel() string {
    94→	s, err := settings.Load()
    95→	if err != nil {
    96→		return ""
    97→	}
    98→	return s.LogLevel
    99→}
   100→
   101→// GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled returns names of agents that have hooks installed.
   102→func GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled() []agent.AgentName {
   103→	var installed []agent.AgentName
   104→	for _, name := range agent.List() {
   105→		ag, err := agent.Get(name)
   106→		if err != nil {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		if hs, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport); ok && hs.AreHooksInstalled() {
   110→			installed = append(installed, name)
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→	return installed
   114→}
   115→
   116→// JoinAgentNames joins agent names into a comma-separated string.
   117→func JoinAgentNames(names []agent.AgentName) string {
   118→	strs := make([]string, len(names))
   119→	for i, n := range names {
   120→		strs[i] = string(n)
   121→	}
   122→	return strings.Join(strs, ",")
   123→}
