     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// Session state management functions shared across all strategies.
    18→// SessionState is stored in .git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json
    19→
    20→// getSessionStateDir returns the path to the session state directory.
    21→// This is stored in the git common dir so it's shared across all worktrees.
    22→func getSessionStateDir() (string, error) {
    23→	commonDir, err := GetGitCommonDir()
    24→	if err != nil {
    25→		return "", err
    26→	}
    27→	return filepath.Join(commonDir, session.SessionStateDirName), nil
    28→}
    29→
    30→// sessionStateFile returns the path to a session state file.
    31→func sessionStateFile(sessionID string) (string, error) {
    32→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		return "", err
    35→	}
    36→	return filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json"), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// LoadSessionState loads the session state for the given session ID.
    40→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
    41→func LoadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    42→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    43→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
    44→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(sessionID)
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID, not user input
    53→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    54→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
    55→	}
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	var state SessionState
    61→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
    62→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
    65→	return &state, nil
    66→}
    67→
    68→// SaveSessionState saves the session state atomically.
    69→func SaveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    70→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    71→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
    72→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    76→	if err != nil {
    77→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state directory: %w", err)
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(state.SessionID)
    90→	if err != nil {
    91→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
    95→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
    96→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
    97→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
    98→	}
    99→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   100→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   101→	}
   102→	return nil
   103→}
   104→
   105→// ListSessionStates returns all session states from the state directory.
   106→// This is a package-level function that doesn't require a specific strategy instance.
   107→func ListSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
   108→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   114→	if err != nil {
   115→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   116→	}
   117→	return states, nil
   118→}
   119→
   120→// FindMostRecentSession returns the session ID of the most recently interacted session
   121→// (by LastInteractionTime) in the current worktree. Returns empty string if no sessions exist.
   122→// Scoping to the current worktree prevents cross-worktree pollution in log routing.
   123→// Falls back to unfiltered search if the worktree path can't be determined.
   124→func FindMostRecentSession() string {
   125→	states, err := ListSessionStates()
   126→	if err != nil || len(states) == 0 {
   127→		return ""
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	// Scope to current worktree to prevent cross-worktree pollution.
   131→	worktreePath, wpErr := GetWorktreePath()
   132→	if wpErr == nil && worktreePath != "" {
   133→		var filtered []*SessionState
   134→		for _, s := range states {
   135→			if s.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   136→				filtered = append(filtered, s)
   137→			}
   138→		}
   139→		if len(filtered) > 0 {
   140→			states = filtered
   141→		}
   142→		// If no sessions match the worktree, fall back to all sessions
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	var best *SessionState
   146→	for _, s := range states {
   147→		if s.LastInteractionTime == nil {
   148→			continue
   149→		}
   150→		if best == nil || s.LastInteractionTime.After(*best.LastInteractionTime) {
   151→			best = s
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→	if best != nil {
   155→		return best.SessionID
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Fallback: return most recently started session
   159→	for _, s := range states {
   160→		if best == nil || s.StartedAt.After(best.StartedAt) {
   161→			best = s
   162→		}
   163→	}
   164→	if best != nil {
   165→		return best.SessionID
   166→	}
   167→	return ""
   168→}
   169→
   170→// TransitionAndLog runs a session phase transition, applies common actions, logs the
   171→// transition, and returns any remaining strategy-specific actions.
   172→// This is the single entry point for all state machine transitions to ensure
   173→// consistent logging of phase changes.
   174→func TransitionAndLog(state *SessionState, event session.Event, ctx session.TransitionContext) []session.Action {
   175→	oldPhase := state.Phase
   176→	result := session.Transition(oldPhase, event, ctx)
   177→	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   178→
   179→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "session")
   180→	if result.NewPhase != oldPhase {
   181→		logging.Info(logCtx, "phase transition",
   182→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   183→			slog.String("event", event.String()),
   184→			slog.String("from", string(oldPhase)),
   185→			slog.String("to", string(result.NewPhase)),
   186→		)
   187→	} else {
   188→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "phase unchanged",
   189→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   190→			slog.String("event", event.String()),
   191→			slog.String("phase", string(result.NewPhase)),
   192→		)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	return remaining
   196→}
   197→
   198→// ClearSessionState removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   199→func ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   200→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   201→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   202→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(sessionID)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   211→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   212→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   213→		}
   214→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   215→	}
   216→	return nil
   217→}
   218→

    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// CLIVersion is the version of the CLI that created this session
    31→	CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
    32→
    33→	// BaseCommit tracks the current shadow branch base. Initially set to HEAD when the
    34→	// session starts, but updated on migration (pull/rebase) and after condensation.
    35→	// Used for shadow branch naming and checkpoint storage — NOT for attribution.
    36→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    37→
    38→	// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
    39→	// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
    40→	// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
    41→	// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
    42→	AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    45→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    46→
    47→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    48→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    49→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    50→
    51→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    52→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    53→
    54→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    55→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    56→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    57→
    58→	// Phase is the lifecycle stage of this session (see phase.go).
    59→	// Empty means idle (backward compat with pre-state-machine files).
    60→	Phase Phase `json:"phase,omitempty"`

`json:\"repo_root\"`\n\tModifiedFiles []string `json:\"modified_files\"`\n\tNewFiles      []string `json:\"new_files\"`\n\tDeletedFiles  []string `json:\"deleted_files\"`\n\tPrompts       []string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tFilesHash     string    `json:\"files_hash\"`\n\tReviewedAt    time.Time `json:\"reviewed_at\"`\n\tReviewApplied bool      `json:\"review_applied\"`\n}\n\nconst (\n\twingmanStateFile  = \".entire/wingman-state.json\"\n\twingmanReviewFile = \".entire/REVIEW.md\"\n)\n\nfunc newWingmanCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"wingman\",\n\t\tShort: \"Automated code review for agent sessions\",\n\t\tLong: `Wingman provides automated code review after agent turns.\n\nWhen enabled, wingman automatically reviews code changes made by agents,\nwrites suggestions to .entire/REVIEW.md, and optionally triggers the agent\nto apply them.`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn cmd.Help()\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newWingmanEnableCmd())\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newWingmanDisableCmd())\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newWingmanStatusCmd())\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newWingmanReviewCmd())\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc newWingmanEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"enable\",\n\t\tShort: \"Enable wingman auto-review\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\tif _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), \"Not a git repository. Please run from within a git repository.\")\n\t\t\t\treturn NewSilentError(errors.New(\"not a git repository\"))\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\ts, err := settings.Load()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif !s.Enabled {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), \"Entire is not enabled. Run 'entire enable' first.\")\n\t\t\t\treturn NewSilentError(errors.New(\"entire not enabled\"))\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif s.StrategyOptions == nil {\n\t\t\t\ts.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ts.StrategyOptions[\"wingman\"] = map[string]any{\"enabled\": true}\n\n\t\t\tif err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to save settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Wingman enabled. Code changes will be automatically reviewed after agent turns.\")\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc newWingmanDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"disable\",\n\t\tShort: \"Disable wingman auto-review\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\ts, err := settings.Load()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif s.StrategyOptions == nil {\n\t\t\t\ts.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ts.StrategyOptions[\"wingman\"] = map[string]any{\"enabled\": false}\n\n\t\t\tif err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to save settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Clean up pending review file if it exists\n\t\t\treviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)\n\t\t\tif err == nil {\n\t\t\t\tos.Remove(reviewPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Wingman disabled.\")\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"status\",\n\t\tShort: \"Show wingman status\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\ts, err := settings.Load()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to load settings: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif s.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Wingman: enabled\")\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Wingman: disabled\")\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Show last review info if available\n\t\t\tstate, err := loadWingmanState()\n\t\t\tif err == nil && state != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Last review: %s\\n\", state.ReviewedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\t\t\t\tif state.ReviewApplied {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Status: applied\")\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Status: pending\")\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Check for pending REVIEW.md\n\t\t\treviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)\n\t\t\tif err == nil {\n\t\t\t\tif _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), \"Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md\")\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\n// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.\nfunc triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {\n\trepoRoot := payload.RepoRoot\n\n\t// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\tfilesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)\n\n\tstate, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup\n\tif state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Marshal payload\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}\n\n// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.\nfunc computeFilesHash(files []string) string {\n\tsorted := make([]string, len(files))\n\tcopy(sorted, files)\n\tsort.Strings(sorted)\n\th := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, \"\\n\")))\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%x\", h)\n}\n\n// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.\nfunc loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {\n\tstatePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tvar state WingmanState\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &state, nil\n}\n\n// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.\nfunc saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {\n\tstatePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tdir := filepath.Dir(statePath)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets\n\treturn os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go",
  "content": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// maxDiffSize is the maximum size of the diff included in the review prompt.\n// Large diffs degrade review quality, so we truncate.\nconst maxDiffSize = 100 * 1024 // 100KB\n\n// reviewPromptTemplate is the prompt sent to Claude for code review.\n//\n// Security note: User content (diff, prompts) is wrapped in XML tags to provide\n// clear boundary markers, similar to the summarization prompt pattern.\nconst reviewPromptTemplate = `You are a senior code reviewer. Review the following code changes and provide actionable feedback.\n\n## Context\n\nThe developer's intent (from their prompts):\n<prompts>\n%s\n</prompts>\n\n## Code Changes\n\nFiles changed: %s\n\n<diff>\n%s\n</diff>\n\n## Instructions\n\nReview the code changes above. Focus on:\n- Bugs or logic errors\n- Security vulnerabilities\n- Performance issues\n- Missing error handling\n- Code style and readability issues\n\nFor each issue found, provide:\n1. Severity: CRITICAL, WARNING, or SUGGESTION\n2. File path and line number (from the diff)\n3. Description of the issue\n4. Suggested fix\n\nFormat your response as Markdown with this structure:\n\n# Code Review\n\n## Summary\nBrief overview of the changes and overall assessment.\n\n## Issues\n\n### [SEVERITY] Short description\n**File:** ` + \"`path/to/file.go:42`\" + `\n**Description:** What the issue is and why it matters.\n**Suggestion:**\n` + \"```\" + `\n// suggested fix\n` + \"```\" + `\n\nIf no issues are found, say so and optionally provide suggestions for improvement.\nDo NOT include any preamble or explanation outside the Markdown structure above.`\n\n// buildReviewPrompt constructs the review prompt from the payload and diff.\nfunc buildReviewPrompt(prompts []string, fileList string, diff string) string {\n\tpromptText := strings.Join(prompts, \"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\")\n\tif promptText == \"\" {\n\t\tpromptText = \"(no prompts captured)\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Truncate large diffs\n\tif len(diff) > maxDiffSize {\n\t\tdiff = diff[:maxDiffSize] + \"\\n\\n... (diff truncated at 100KB)\"\n\t}\n\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(reviewPromptTemplate, promptText, fileList, diff)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go",
  "content": "//go:build unix\n\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n)\n\n// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.\n// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues\n// after the parent exits.\nfunc spawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) {\n\texecutable, err := os.Executable()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadJSON is controlled internally, not user input\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, \"wingman\", \"__review\", payloadJSON)\n\n\t// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{\n\t\tSetpgid: true,\n\t}\n\n\t// Don't hold the working directory\n\tcmd.Dir = \"/\"\n\n\t// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)\n\tcmd.Env = os.Environ()\n\n\t// Discard stdout/stderr to prevent output leaking to parent's terminal\n\tcmd.Stdout = io.Discard\n\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\n\t// Start the process (non-blocking)\n\tif err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Release the process so it can run independently\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}\n"
}

  "content": "//go:build !unix\n\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging\"\n)\n\n// spawnDetachedWingmanReview is a no-op on non-Unix platforms.\n// Windows support for detached processes would require different syscall flags\n// (CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP, DETACHED_PROCESS), but wingman is best-effort\n// so we simply skip it on unsupported platforms.\nfunc spawnDetachedWingmanReview(string) {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"wingman\")\n\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"wingman review not supported on this platform\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"reason\", \"detached subprocess spawning not implemented\"),\n\t)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Not supported on this platform\\n\")\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go",
  "content": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\t// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,\n\t// letting the agent turn fully settle.\n\twingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second\n\n\t// wingmanApplyDelay is how long to wait after writing REVIEW.md\n\t// before attempting to auto-apply.\n\twingmanApplyDelay = 30 * time.Second\n\n\t// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.\n\twingmanReviewModel = \"sonnet\"\n)\n\n// claudeCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.\ntype wingmanCLIResponse struct {\n\tResult string `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\nfunc newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\treturn &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:    \"__review\",\n\t\tHidden: true,\n\t\tArgs:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),\n\t\tRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runWingmanReview(args[0])\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc runWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) error {\n\tvar payload WingmanPayload\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(payloadJSON), &payload); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to unmarshal payload: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trepoRoot := payload.RepoRoot\n\tif repoRoot == \"\" {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"repo_root is required in payload\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle\n\ttime.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)\n\n\t// Compute diff\n\tdiff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to compute diff: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(diff) == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil // No changes to review\n\t}\n\n\t// Build file list for the prompt\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tfor _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {\n\t\tallFiles = append(allFiles, f+\" (modified)\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, f := range payload.NewFiles {\n\t\tallFiles = append(allFiles, f+\" (new)\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {\n\t\tallFiles = append(allFiles, f+\" (deleted)\")\n\t}\n\tfileList := strings.Join(allFiles, \", \")\n\n\t// Build review prompt\n\tprompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, fileList, diff)\n\n\t// Call Claude CLI for review\n\treviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get review from Claude: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write REVIEW.md\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write REVIEW.md: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update dedup state\n\tallFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\n\t// Save state from repo root context\n\tif err := os.Chdir(repoRoot); err == nil {\n\t\t_ = saveWingmanState(&WingmanState{ //nolint:errcheck // best-effort state save\n\t\t\tSessionID:     payload.SessionID,\n\t\t\tFilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),\n\t\t\tReviewedAt:    time.Now(),\n\t\t\tReviewApplied: false,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle\n\ttime.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)\n\n\tif !isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID) {\n\t\treturn nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification\n\t}\n\n\t// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// computeDiff gets the current uncommitted diff in the repo.\nfunc computeDiff(repoRoot string) (string, error) {\n\t// Try uncommitted changes first\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot comes from our own payload\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", \"diff\", \"HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = repoRoot\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t// If HEAD doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot comes from our own payload\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.Command(\"git\", \"diff\")\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)\", err, stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t}\n\n\t// If no uncommitted changes, check if the last commit was from auto-commit strategy\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == \"\" {\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G204: repoRoot comes from our own payload\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.Command(\"git\", \"diff\", \"HEAD~1\", \"HEAD\")\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn stdout.String(), nil\n}\n\n// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.\nfunc callClaudeForReview(prompt string) (string, error) {\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: claude path is hardcoded\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"claude\", \"--print\", \"--output-format\", \"json\", \"--model\", wingmanReviewModel, \"--setting-sources\", \"\")\n\n\t// Isolate from git repo to prevent hooks and index pollution\n\tcmd.Dir = os.TempDir()\n\tcmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())\n\n\tcmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tvar execErr *exec.Error\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &execErr) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI not found: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar exitErr *exec.ExitError\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s\", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to run claude CLI: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse the CLI response\n\tvar cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse claude CLI response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn cliResponse.Result, nil\n}\n\n// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.\nfunc isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {\n\tstate, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil || state == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle\n}\n\n// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.\nfunc triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {\n\tapplyPrompt := \"Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion that you agree with. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md.\"\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: claude path is hardcoded\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"claude\", \"--continue\", \"-p\", applyPrompt, \"--setting-sources\", \"\")\n\tcmd.Dir = repoRoot\n\t// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, but keep other env\n\tcmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\treturn cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps\n}\n\n// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.\n// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo\n// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).\nfunc wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {\n\tfiltered := make([]string, 0, len(env))\n\tfor _, e := range env {\n\t\tif !strings.HasPrefix(e, \"GIT_\") {\n\t\t\tfiltered = append(filtered, e)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn filtered\n}\n"
}

3. `wrapcheck` - need to wrap errors from external packages

    30→// WingmanState tracks deduplication and review state.
    31→type WingmanState struct {
    32→	SessionID     string    `json:"session_id"`
    33→	FilesHash     string    `json:"files_hash"`
    34→	ReviewedAt    time.Time `json:"reviewed_at"`
    35→	ReviewApplied bool      `json:"review_applied"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→const (
    39→	wingmanStateFile  = ".entire/wingman-state.json"
    40→	wingmanReviewFile = ".entire/REVIEW.md"
    41→)
    42→
    43→func newWingmanCmd() *cobra.Command {
    44→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    45→		Use:   "wingman",
    46→		Short: "Automated code review for agent sessions",
    47→		Long: `Wingman provides automated code review after agent turns.
    48→
    49→When enabled, wingman automatically reviews code changes made by agents,
    50→writes suggestions to .entire/REVIEW.md, and optionally triggers the agent
    51→to apply them.`,
    52→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    53→			return cmd.Help()
    54→		},
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanEnableCmd())
    58→	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanDisableCmd())
    59→	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanStatusCmd())
    60→	cmd.AddCommand(newWingmanReviewCmd())
    61→
    62→	return cmd
    63→}
    64→
    65→func newWingmanEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
    66→	return &cobra.Command{
    67→		Use:   "enable",
    68→		Short: "Enable wingman auto-review",
    69→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    70→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    71→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run from within a git repository.")
    72→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
    73→			}
    74→
    75→			s, err := settings.Load()
    76→			if err != nil {
    77→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    78→			}
    79→
    80→			if !s.Enabled {
    81→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Entire is not enabled. Run 'entire enable' first.")
    82→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("entire not enabled"))
    83→			}
    84→
    85→			if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
    86→				s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
    87→			}
    88→			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": true}
    89→
    90→			if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
    91→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
    92→			}
    93→
    94→			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman enabled. Code changes will be automatically reviewed after agent turns.")
    95→			return nil
    96→		},
    97→	}
    98→}
    99→
   100→func newWingmanDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   101→	return &cobra.Command{
   102→		Use:   "disable",
   103→		Short: "Disable wingman auto-review",
   104→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   105→			s, err := settings.Load()
   106→			if err != nil {
   107→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   108→			}
   109→
   110→			if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   111→				s.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]any)
   112→			}
   113→			s.StrategyOptions["wingman"] = map[string]any{"enabled": false}
   114→
   115→			if err := settings.Save(s); err != nil {
   116→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   117→			}
   118→
   119→			// Clean up pending review file if it exists
   120→			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
   121→			if err == nil {
   122→				os.Remove(reviewPath)
   123→			}
   124→
   125→			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman disabled.")
   126→			return nil
   127→		},
   128→	}
   129→}
   130→
   131→func newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
   132→	return &cobra.Command{
   133→		Use:   "status",
   134→		Short: "Show wingman status",
   135→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   136→			s, err := settings.Load()
   137→			if err != nil {
   138→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   139→			}
   140→
   141→			if s.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   142→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: enabled")
   143→			} else {
   144→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: disabled")
   145→			}
   146→
   147→			// Show last review info if available
   148→			state, err := loadWingmanState()
   149→			if err == nil && state != nil {
   150→				fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Last review: %s\n", state.ReviewedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   151→				if state.ReviewApplied {
   152→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: applied")
   153→				} else {
   154→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: pending")
   155→				}
   156→			}
   157→
   158→			// Check for pending REVIEW.md
   159→			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
   160→			if err == nil {
   161→				if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {
   162→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   163→				}
   164→			}
   165→
   166→			return nil
   167→		},
   168→	}
   169→}
   170→
   171→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   172→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   173→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   174→
   175→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   176→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   177→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   178→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   179→		return
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   183→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   184→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   185→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   186→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   187→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   188→
   189→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   190→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   191→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   192→		return
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Marshal payload
   196→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   199→		return
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Spawn detached review process
   203→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))
   204→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   205→}
   206→
   207→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   208→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   209→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   210→	copy(sorted, files)
   211→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   212→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\n")))
   213→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   214→}
   215→
   216→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   217→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   218→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   219→	if err != nil {
   220→		return nil, err
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   224→	if err != nil {
   225→		return nil, err
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	var state WingmanState
   229→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   230→		return nil, err
   231→	}
   232→	return &state, nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   236→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   237→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   238→	if err != nil {
   239→		return err
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   243→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   244→		return err
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   248→	if err != nil {
   249→		return err
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   253→	return os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   254→}
   255→
```

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"path/filepath"
    11→	"strings"
    12→	"time"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    16→	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
    17→)
    18→
    19→const (
    20→	// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,
    21→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.
    22→	wingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second
    23→
    24→	// wingmanApplyDelay is how long to wait after writing REVIEW.md
    25→	// before attempting to auto-apply.
    26→	wingmanApplyDelay = 30 * time.Second
    27→
    28→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    29→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    30→)
    31→
    32→// claudeCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.
    33→type wingmanCLIResponse struct {
    34→	Result string `json:"result"`
    35→}
    36→
    37→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    38→	return &cobra.Command{
    39→		Use:    "__review",
    40→		Hidden: true,
    41→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    42→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    43→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    44→		},
    45→	}
    46→}
    47→
    48→func runWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) error {
    49→	var payload WingmanPayload
    50→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(payloadJSON), &payload); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    55→	if repoRoot == "" {
    56→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
    60→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
    61→
    62→	// Compute diff
    63→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot)
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
    69→		return nil // No changes to review
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Build file list for the prompt
    73→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    74→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
    75→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
    76→	}
    77→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
    78→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
    79→	}
    80→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
    81→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
    82→	}
    83→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
    84→
    85→	// Build review prompt
    86→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, fileList, diff)
    87→
    88→	// Call Claude CLI for review
    89→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)
    90→	if err != nil {
    91→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Write REVIEW.md
    95→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
    96→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
    97→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
    98→	}
    99→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   100→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// Update dedup state
   104→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   105→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   106→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   107→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   108→
   109→	// Save state from repo root context
   110→	if err := os.Chdir(repoRoot); err == nil {
   111→		_ = saveWingmanState(&WingmanState{ //nolint:errcheck // best-effort state save
   112→			SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   113→			FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   114→			ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   115→			ReviewApplied: false,
   116→		})
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle
   120→	time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   121→
   122→	if !isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID) {
   123→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue
   127→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   128→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil
   132→}
   133→
   134→// computeDiff gets the current uncommitted diff in the repo.
   135→func computeDiff(repoRoot string) (string, error) {
   136→	// Try uncommitted changes first
   137→
   138→	cmd := exec.Command("git", "diff", "HEAD")
   139→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   140→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   141→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   142→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   143→
   144→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   145→		// If HEAD doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged
   146→
   147→		cmd2 := exec.Command("git", "diff")
   148→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   149→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   150→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   151→		if err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {
   152→			return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)", err, stderr.String())
   153→		}
   154→		return stdout2.String(), nil
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// If no uncommitted changes, check if the last commit was from auto-commit strategy
   158→	if strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == "" {
   159→
   160→		cmd2 := exec.Command("git", "diff", "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   161→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   162→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   163→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   164→		if err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != "" {
   165→			return stdout2.String(), nil
   166→		}
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	return stdout.String(), nil
   170→}
   171→
   172→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   173→func callClaudeForReview(prompt string) (string, error) {
   174→
   175→	cmd := exec.Command("claude", "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", wingmanReviewModel, "--setting-sources", "")
   176→
   177→	// Isolate from git repo to prevent hooks and index pollution
   178→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   179→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   180→
   181→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   182→
   183→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   184→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   185→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   186→
   187→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   188→		var execErr *exec.Error
   189→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   190→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   191→		}
   192→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   193→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   194→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   195→		}
   196→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Parse the CLI response
   200→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   201→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   202→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   209→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   210→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   212→		return false
   213→	}
   214→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   215→}
   216→
   217→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   218→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   219→	applyPrompt := "Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion that you agree with. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md."
   220→
   221→	cmd := exec.Command("claude", "--continue", "-p", applyPrompt, "--setting-sources", "")
   222→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   223→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, but keep other env
   224→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   225→
   226→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   227→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   228→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   229→
   230→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   231→}
   232→
   233→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   234→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   235→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   236→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   237→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   238→	for _, e := range env {
   239→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   240→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   241→		}
   242→	}
   243→	return filtered
   244→}
   245→

The issue is `paths.AbsPath` requires a real git repo (it calls `git rev-parse`). I need to use `git init` instead of just creating a `.git` directory. Let me fix this test:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01EJgB2jrgCRWNEtf5owGsBN
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go",
  "old_string": "func TestWingmanState_SaveLoad(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\n\t// Create .entire and .git directories\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .entire directory: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".git\"), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .git directory: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt.Chdir(tmpDir)\n\n\tstate := &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:     \"test-session\",\n\t\tFilesHash:     \"abc123\",\n\t\tReviewApplied: false,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := saveWingmanState(state); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to save state: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tloaded, err := loadWingmanState()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to load state: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif loaded.SessionID != state.SessionID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"session_id: got %q, want %q\", loaded.SessionID, state.SessionID)\n\t}\n\tif loaded.FilesHash != state.FilesHash {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"files_hash: got %q, want %q\", loaded.FilesHash, state.FilesHash)\n\t}\n\tif loaded.ReviewApplied != state.ReviewApplied {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"review_applied: got %v, want %v\", loaded.ReviewApplied, state.ReviewApplied)\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "func TestWingmanState_SaveLoad(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Uses t.Chdir so cannot be parallel\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\n\t// Initialize a real git repo (paths.AbsPath needs git rev-parse)\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", \"init\", tmpDir)\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to git init: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create .entire directory\n\tentireDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".entire\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .entire directory: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt.Chdir(tmpDir)\n\n\tstate := &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:     \"test-session\",\n\t\tFilesHash:     \"abc123\",\n\t\tReviewApplied: false,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := saveWingmanState(state); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to save state: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tloaded, err := loadWingmanState()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to load state: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif loaded.SessionID != state.SessionID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"session_id: got %q, want %q\", loaded.SessionID, state.SessionID)\n\t}\n\tif loaded.FilesHash != state.FilesHash {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"files_hash: got %q, want %q\", loaded.FilesHash, state.FilesHash)\n\t}\n\tif loaded.ReviewApplied != state.ReviewApplied {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"review_applied: got %v, want %v\", loaded.ReviewApplied, state.ReviewApplied)\n\t}\n}"
}

> AGENT

All checks pass. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Implementation Summary

### Files Created (6)
| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | Command group (`entire wingman enable/disable/status`), payload types, trigger logic, dedup, state management |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Hidden `__review` command — detached worker that computes diff, calls Claude for review, writes REVIEW.md, auto-applies after delay |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go` | Review prompt template for Claude |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached process spawner for Unix (process group detachment) |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | No-op stub for non-Unix platforms |
| `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Unit tests (13 tests: hash dedup, payload roundtrip, prompt building, state save/load, settings parsing, env stripping) |

### Files Modified (3)
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go` | Added `IsWingmanEnabled()` method + package-level convenience function |
| `cmd/entire/cli/root.go` | Registered `newWingmanCmd()` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Added wingman trigger in stop hook + REVIEW.md notification in prompt-submit hook |

### Key Design Decisions
- **Loop breaking**: `--setting-sources ""` on auto-apply `claude --continue` disables all hooks, preventing infinite review loops
- **Dedup**: SHA256 hash of sorted file paths prevents re-reviewing identical changes
- **Race protection**: 30s delay + session phase check (IDLE) before auto-apply; skips if user is active
- **Isolation**: `wingmanStripGitEnv()` + `cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()` prevents Claude from polluting the git index during review
- **Follows existing patterns**: detached spawner mirrors `telemetry/detached_unix.go`, Claude CLI invocation mirrors `summarize/claude.go`, settings follow `IsSummarizeEnabled()` pattern

+// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
+func IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
+	s, err := Load()
+	if err != nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	return s.IsWingmanEnabled()
+}
+
+// IsWingmanEnabled checks if wingman auto-review is enabled in this settings instance.
+func (s *EntireSettings) IsWingmanEnabled() bool {
+	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
+		return false
+	}
+	wingmanOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["wingman"].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	enabled, ok := wingmanOpts["enabled"].(bool)
+	if !ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	return enabled
+}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/.entire/WINGMAN_PLAN.md",
  "content": "# Entire Wingman - Implementation Plan\n\n## Context\n\nThe wingman feature adds an automated code review loop to the CLI. After an agent turn that modifies files, a background reviewer analyzes the changes (using checkpoint data to understand intent), writes suggestions to `.entire/REVIEW.md`, and then — after a short delay — triggers the main agent to apply those suggestions via `claude --continue -p`. This creates a self-improving development loop where agent output gets automatically reviewed and refined.\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\n```\nAgent turn ends (stop hook)\n    │\n    ├── SaveChanges (existing)\n    ├── transitionSessionTurnEnd (existing)\n    └── [NEW] If wingman enabled && files changed && no pending REVIEW.md && not already reviewed:\n        │\n        └── Spawn detached: `entire wingman __review <json>`\n            │\n            ├── Sleep ~10s (let agent turn fully settle)\n            ├── git diff HEAD (get code changes)\n            ├── Read user prompts from payload (for intent)\n            ├── claude --print --model sonnet (review)\n            ├── Write .entire/REVIEW.md\n            └── Wait ~30s, then if session still IDLE:\n                └── claude --continue -p \"Apply .entire/REVIEW.md\" --setting-sources \"\"\n                    │\n                    └── Agent applies suggestions, deletes REVIEW.md\n                        (no hooks fire due to --setting-sources \"\",\n                         so no infinite loop)\n```\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### 1. Settings support\n\n**File: `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`**\n\nAdd `IsWingmanEnabled()` method + package-level convenience function, following the `IsSummarizeEnabled()` pattern. Settings path: `strategy_options.wingman.enabled`.\n\n### 2. `entire wingman` command group\n\n**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go`**\n\nCobra command group with subcommands:\n- `entire wingman enable` — Sets `strategy_options.wingman.enabled = true` in settings. Validates Entire is enabled first.\n- `entire wingman disable` — Sets it to `false`. Deletes `.entire/REVIEW.md` if present.\n- `entire wingman status` — Shows enabled state + last review timestamp from `.entire/wingman-state.json`.\n\n**File: `cmd/entire/cli/root.go`** — Register `newWingmanCmd()`.\n\n### 3. Hidden `__review` command (detached worker)\n\n**Within `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go`**:\n\nHidden command: `entire wingman __review <json-payload>`\n\nSteps:\n1. Unmarshal `WingmanPayload` (session ID, repo root, modified/new/deleted files, user prompts, commit message)\n2. `cd` to repo root\n3. **Initial delay**: Sleep ~10s to let the agent turn fully settle\n4. Compute diff: `git diff HEAD` for uncommitted changes, or `git diff HEAD~1 HEAD` if auto-commit strategy just committed\n5. Build review prompt (diff + prompts + file list)\n6. Call `claude --print --model sonnet --output-format json --setting-sources \"\"` with prompt via stdin (reuse `stripGitEnv` pattern from `summarize/claude.go`, run from `os.TempDir()`)\n7. Parse response, extract review text\n8. Write `.entire/REVIEW.md`\n9. Update `.entire/wingman-state.json` (dedup tracking)\n10. **Auto-apply phase**: Sleep ~30s, then:\n    - Load session state, check phase is `IDLE`\n    - If idle: spawn `claude --continue -p \"Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md.\" --setting-sources \"\"`\n    - `--setting-sources \"\"` disables all hooks on the automated process — prevents recursive wingman triggers and session state conflicts\n    - If not idle (user is active): exit, leave REVIEW.md for notification\n\n### 4. Detached subprocess spawner\n\n**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go`** (`//go:build unix`)\n**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go`** (`//go:build !unix`)\n\nFollow exact pattern from `telemetry/detached_unix.go`:\n- `os.Executable()` to get own binary\n- `Setpgid: true` for process group detachment\n- `cmd.Start()` + `Process.Release()`\n- Non-unix stub is a no-op\n\n### 5. Hook integration (stop hook trigger)\n\n**File: `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**\n\nAfter `transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)` in `commitWithMetadata()`:\n\n```go\nif totalChanges > 0 && settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n    triggerWingmanReview(WingmanPayload{\n        SessionID:     sessionID,\n        RepoRoot:      repoRoot,\n        ModifiedFiles: relModifiedFiles,\n        NewFiles:      relNewFiles,\n        DeletedFiles:  relDeletedFiles,\n        Prompts:       allPrompts,\n        CommitMessage: commitMessage,\n    })\n}\n```\n\n`triggerWingmanReview` (in wingman.go):\n1. Check if `.entire/REVIEW.md` already exists — skip if a pending review hasn't been applied yet\n2. Check deduplication: compute SHA256 of sorted file paths, compare against `.entire/wingman-state.json`. Skip if same hash.\n3. Marshal payload to JSON\n4. Call `spawnDetachedWingmanReview(jsonStr)`\n5. Print to stderr: `\"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\"`\n\n### 6. Notification on next prompt (fallback)\n\n**File: `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`**\n\nIn `captureInitialState()`, after existing logic:\n\n```go\nif settings.IsWingmanEnabled() {\n    if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"REVIEW.md\")); err == nil {\n        fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review available: .entire/REVIEW.md — review and apply suggestions\\n\")\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 7. Review prompt template\n\n**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go`**\n\nPrompt instructs Claude to act as a senior code reviewer. Key elements:\n- User's intent (from prompts/commit message)\n- Full code diff\n- File change list\n- Output format: structured Markdown with severity levels (CRITICAL / WARNING / SUGGESTION), file paths, line numbers, descriptions, and fix suggestions\n- Diff truncated at ~100KB to keep review quality high\n\n### 8. Deduplication & state tracking\n\n**State file: `.entire/wingman-state.json`**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"session_id\": \"...\",\n  \"files_hash\": \"sha256-of-sorted-file-paths\",\n  \"reviewed_at\": \"2026-02-11T...\",\n  \"review_applied\": false\n}\n```\n\n- Before spawning review: skip if `files_hash` matches current\n- After writing REVIEW.md: update state\n- `.entire/wingman-state.json` is already inside `.entire/` (gitignored)\n\n### 9. Loop-breaking mechanism\n\nThe `--setting-sources \"\"` flag on the auto-apply `claude --continue` call disables all Claude Code hooks (including Entire's stop hook). This means:\n- No `commitWithMetadata()` fires after the auto-apply turn\n- No wingman trigger, no infinite loop\n- The file edits are still in the working directory\n- The next human-initiated turn will checkpoint them normally\n\n### 10. Tests\n\n**New file: `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go`**\n- Settings parsing (`IsWingmanEnabled` with various configs)\n- Dedup logic (files hash computation, skip-if-same-hash)\n- Review prompt building (correct template interpolation, diff truncation)\n- Payload serialization/deserialization round-trip\n- State save/load round-trip\n- `wingmanStripGitEnv` filtering\n\n## Files Summary\n\n| Action | File | Purpose |\n|--------|------|---------|\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go` | Command group, payload types, trigger, dedup, state management |\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_prompt.go` | Review prompt template |\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go` | Hidden __review command, diff computation, Claude invocation, auto-apply |\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_unix.go` | Detached process spawner (unix) |\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_spawn_other.go` | Detached process spawner (stub) |\n| Create | `cmd/entire/cli/wingman_test.go` | Unit tests |\n| Modify | `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go` | Add `IsWingmanEnabled()` |\n| Modify | `cmd/entire/cli/root.go` | Register `newWingmanCmd()` |\n| Modify | `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` | Trigger wingman + REVIEW.md notification |\n\n## Existing code to reuse\n\n- `summarize/claude.go` — `stripGitEnv()` pattern, Claude CLI invocation pattern, JSON response parsing\n- `telemetry/detached_unix.go` — detached subprocess spawn pattern\n- `settings/settings.go` — `IsSummarizeEnabled()` pattern for feature flags\n- `session/phase.go` — Phase check (IDLE detection for auto-apply timing)\n- `strategy.LoadSessionState()` — Read session phase from `.git/entire-sessions/`\n\n## Edge cases\n\n- **Claude CLI not installed**: Detached subprocess catches `exec.Error`, logs, exits silently\n- **Large diffs**: Truncate diff at ~100KB in the prompt to keep review quality high\n- **Race with user**: 30s delay + phase check prevents conflicts; if user starts typing, auto-apply is skipped\n- **Multiple concurrent sessions**: Each session has its own session ID in the payload; dedup is per-session\n\n## Current Implementation Status\n\nAll files listed above have been implemented and are in the working tree (unstaged). All pass:\n- `mise run fmt` — clean\n- `mise run lint` — 0 issues\n- `mise run test:ci` — all tests pass (unit + integration + race detection)\n"
}

   152→				if state.ReviewApplied {

   163→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   164→				}
   165→			}
   166→

   171→
   172→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   173→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   174→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   175→
   176→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   177→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   178→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   179→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   180→		return
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   184→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   185→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   186→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   187→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   188→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   189→
   190→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   191→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   192→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   193→		return
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Marshal payload
   197→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   200→		return
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Spawn detached review process
   204→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))
   205→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   206→}
   207→
   208→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   209→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   210→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   211→	copy(sorted, files)
   212→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   213→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\n")))
   214→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   215→}
   216→
   217→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   218→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   219→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	var state WingmanState
   230→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   231→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	return &state, nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   237→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   238→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   244→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→	return nil
   258→}
   259→

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (

    20→const (
    21→	// wingmanInitialDelay is how long to wait before starting the review,
    22→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.
    23→	wingmanInitialDelay = 10 * time.Second
    24→
    25→	// wingmanApplyDelay is how long to wait after writing REVIEW.md
    26→	// before attempting to auto-apply.
    27→	wingmanApplyDelay = 30 * time.Second
    28→
    29→	// wingmanReviewModel is the Claude model used for reviews.
    30→	wingmanReviewModel = "sonnet"
    31→)
    32→
    33→// wingmanCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI --output-format json.
    34→type wingmanCLIResponse struct {
    35→	Result string `json:"result"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    39→	return &cobra.Command{
    40→		Use:    "__review",
    41→		Hidden: true,
    42→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    43→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    44→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    45→		},
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→func runWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) error {
    50→	var payload WingmanPayload
    51→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(payloadJSON), &payload); err != nil {
    52→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    56→	if repoRoot == "" {
    57→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
    61→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
    62→
    63→	// Compute diff
    64→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
    70→		return nil // No changes to review
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Build file list for the prompt
    74→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    75→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
    76→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
    77→	}
    78→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
    79→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
    80→	}
    81→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
    82→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
    83→	}
    84→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
    85→
    86→	// Build review prompt
    87→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, fileList, diff)
    88→
    89→	// Call Claude CLI for review
    90→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Write REVIEW.md
    96→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
    97→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
    98→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   101→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Update dedup state
   106→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   107→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   108→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   109→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   110→
   111→	// Save state from repo root context
   112→	if err := os.Chdir(repoRoot); err == nil {
   113→		_ = saveWingmanState(&WingmanState{ //nolint:errcheck // best-effort state save
   114→			SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   115→			FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   116→			ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   117→			ReviewApplied: false,
   118→		})
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle
   122→	time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   123→
   124→	if !isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID) {
   125→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue
   129→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   130→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	return nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→// computeDiff gets the current uncommitted diff in the repo.
   137→func computeDiff(repoRoot string) (string, error) {
   138→	// Try uncommitted changes first
   139→
   140→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff", "HEAD")
   141→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   142→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   143→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   144→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   145→
   146→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   147→		// If HEAD doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged
   148→
   149→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff")
   150→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   151→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   152→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   153→		if err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {
   154→			return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)", err, stderr.String())
   155→		}
   156→		return stdout2.String(), nil
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	// If no uncommitted changes, check if the last commit was from auto-commit strategy
   160→	if strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == "" {
   161→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff", "HEAD~1", "HEAD")
   162→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   163→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   164→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   165→		if err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != "" {
   166→			return stdout2.String(), nil
   167→		}
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	return stdout.String(), nil
   171→}
   172→
   173→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   174→func callClaudeForReview(prompt string) (string, error) {
   175→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "claude", "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", wingmanReviewModel, "--setting-sources", "")
   176→
   177→	// Isolate from git repo to prevent hooks and index pollution
   178→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   179→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   180→
   181→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   182→
   183→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   184→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   185→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   186→
   187→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   188→		var execErr *exec.Error
   189→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   190→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   191→		}
   192→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   193→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   194→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   195→		}
   196→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Parse the CLI response
   200→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   201→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   202→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   209→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   210→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   212→		return false
   213→	}
   214→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   215→}
   216→
   217→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   218→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   219→	applyPrompt := "Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion that you agree with. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md."
   220→
   221→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "claude", "--continue", "-p", applyPrompt, "--setting-sources", "")
   222→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   223→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, but keep other env
   224→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   225→
   226→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   227→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   228→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   229→
   230→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   231→}
   232→
   233→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   234→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   235→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   236→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   237→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   238→	for _, e := range env {
   239→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   240→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   241→		}
   242→	}
   243→	return filtered
   244→}
   245→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go",
  "old_string": "// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.\nfunc triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {\n\trepoRoot := payload.RepoRoot\n\n\t// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\tfilesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)\n\n\tstate, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup\n\tif state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Marshal payload\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.\nfunc triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {\n\trepoRoot := payload.RepoRoot\n\n\t// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. Multiple rapid stop hooks\n\t// could race past the dedup check (which reads stale state) before any\n\t// review completes and updates the state file.\n\tlockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(lockPath); err == nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\tfilesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)\n\n\tstate, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup\n\tif state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against\n\t// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.\n\tpayload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)\n\n\t// Write lock file synchronously before spawning to prevent races\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: lock file is not secrets\n\t_ = os.WriteFile(lockPath, []byte(payload.SessionID), 0o644) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort lock\n\n\t// Marshal payload\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}\n\n// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.\nfunc resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = repoRoot\n\tout, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(out))\n}"
}

{
