  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman_review.go",
  "old_string": "// computeDiff gets the current uncommitted diff in the repo.\nfunc computeDiff(repoRoot string) (string, error) {\n\t// Try uncommitted changes first\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\", \"HEAD\")\n\tcmd.Dir = repoRoot\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t// If HEAD doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged\n\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\")\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)\", err, stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t}\n\n\t// If no uncommitted changes, check if the last commit was from auto-commit strategy\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == \"\" {\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\", \"HEAD~1\", \"HEAD\")\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn stdout.String(), nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// computeDiff gets the code changes to review. baseCommit is the HEAD hash\n// captured at trigger time so the diff is stable even if HEAD moves during\n// the initial delay (e.g., auto-commit creates another commit, or user commits).\nfunc computeDiff(repoRoot string, baseCommit string) (string, error) {\n\t// Use the captured base commit when available for deterministic diffs\n\tdiffRef := \"HEAD\"\n\tif baseCommit != \"\" {\n\t\tdiffRef = baseCommit\n\t}\n\n\t// Try uncommitted changes against the base commit\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: diffRef is a git hash or \"HEAD\"\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\", diffRef)\n\tcmd.Dir = repoRoot\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t// If the ref doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\")\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)\", err, stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t}\n\n\t// If no uncommitted changes, the commit itself may contain the changes\n\t// (auto-commit strategy creates commits on the active branch)\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == \"\" {\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G204: diffRef is a git hash or \"HEAD\"\n\t\tcmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), \"git\", \"diff\", diffRef+\"~1\", diffRef)\n\t\tcmd2.Dir = repoRoot\n\t\tvar stdout2 bytes.Buffer\n\t\tcmd2.Stdout = &stdout2\n\t\tif err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn stdout2.String(), nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn stdout.String(), nil\n}"
}

> AGENT

All clean — 0 lint issues, all tests pass (unit + integration + race detection).

Both review issues are resolved:

1. **Lock file** (`wingman.go:197-206`): A `.entire/wingman.lock` file is written synchronously before spawning the detached process. Concurrent stop hooks see the lock and skip. The detached process removes it via `defer` when done.

2. **Base commit capture** (`wingman.go:210`, `wingman_review.go:143`): `resolveHEAD()` captures the HEAD hash at trigger time into `payload.BaseCommit`. `computeDiff()` now uses that pinned hash instead of `HEAD`, so the diff is stable even if HEAD moves during the 10s initial delay.

+		return false
+	}

+	if !ok {

+	}

> DEVELOPER

There's new feedback in @.entire/REVIEW.md make sure you review it carefully for validity first before continuing to solve any of it.

> AGENT

Let me evaluate each issue:

1. **[CRITICAL] Lock file TOCTOU race** — Valid. `Stat()` then `WriteFile()` is a classic race. `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` is the atomic fix.

2. **[CRITICAL] No timeouts on Claude CLI calls** — Valid. A hanging `claude` process blocks the lock forever, permanently disabling wingman. Must fix.

3. **[WARNING] Payload as CLI argument can exceed argv limit** — Valid. Long prompts or many files could hit OS limits. Temp file is the right fix.

4. **[WARNING] `os.Chdir` mutates process-wide state** — Valid. Unnecessary when we can write the state file directly to a known path.

5. **[WARNING] Stale lock never cleaned on crash** — Valid. SIGKILL skips `defer`. A modtime-based stale check is a good defensive measure.

6. **[SUGGESTION] Dedup hash newline ambiguity** — Valid but extremely low probability. Trivial fix though, so worth including.

All 6 are legitimate. Let me fix them.

   123→
   124→			// Clean up pending review file if it exists
   125→			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
   126→			if err == nil {
   127→				_ = os.Remove(reviewPath)
   128→			}
   129→
   130→			fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman disabled.")
   131→			return nil
   132→		},
   133→	}
   134→}
   135→
   136→func newWingmanStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
   137→	return &cobra.Command{
   138→		Use:   "status",
   139→		Short: "Show wingman status",
   140→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   141→			s, err := settings.Load()
   142→			if err != nil {
   143→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   144→			}
   145→
   146→			if s.IsWingmanEnabled() {
   147→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: enabled")
   148→			} else {
   149→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Wingman: disabled")
   150→			}
   151→
   152→			// Show last review info if available
   153→			state, err := loadWingmanState()
   154→			if err == nil && state != nil {
   155→				fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Last review: %s\n", state.ReviewedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
   156→				if state.ReviewApplied {
   157→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: applied")
   158→				} else {
   159→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Status: pending")
   160→				}
   161→			}
   162→
   163→			// Check for pending REVIEW.md
   164→			reviewPath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanReviewFile)
   165→			if err == nil {
   166→				if _, statErr := os.Stat(reviewPath); statErr == nil {
   167→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   168→				}
   169→			}
   170→
   171→			return nil
   172→		},
   173→	}
   174→}
   175→
   176→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   177→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   178→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   179→
   180→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   181→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   182→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   183→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   184→		return
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. Multiple rapid stop hooks
   188→	// could race past the dedup check (which reads stale state) before any
   189→	// review completes and updates the state file.
   190→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   191→	if _, err := os.Stat(lockPath); err == nil {
   192→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   193→		return
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   197→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   198→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   199→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   200→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   201→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   202→
   203→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   204→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   205→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   206→		return
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   210→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   211→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   212→
   213→	// Write lock file synchronously before spawning to prevent races
   214→	//nolint:gosec // G306: lock file is not secrets
   215→	_ = os.WriteFile(lockPath, []byte(payload.SessionID), 0o644) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort lock
   216→
   217→	// Marshal payload
   218→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   219→	if err != nil {
   220→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   221→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   222→		return
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	// Spawn detached review process
   226→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))
   227→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   228→}
   229→
   230→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   231→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   232→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   233→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   234→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		return ""
   237→	}
   238→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   239→}
   240→
   241→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   242→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   243→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   244→	copy(sorted, files)
   245→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   246→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\n")))
   247→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   248→}
   249→
   250→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   251→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   252→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   258→	if err != nil {
   259→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	var state WingmanState
   263→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   264→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   265→	}
   266→	return &state, nil
   267→}
   268→
   269→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   270→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   271→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   277→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   278→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   282→	if err != nil {
   283→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   287→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   288→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   289→	}
   290→	return nil
   291→}
   292→

    22→	// letting the agent turn fully settle.

    24→
    25→	// wingmanApplyDelay is how long to wait after writing REVIEW.md
    26→	// before attempting to auto-apply.

    38→func newWingmanReviewCmd() *cobra.Command {
    39→	return &cobra.Command{
    40→		Use:    "__review",
    41→		Hidden: true,
    42→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    43→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    44→			return runWingmanReview(args[0])
    45→		},
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→func runWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) error {
    50→	var payload WingmanPayload
    51→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(payloadJSON), &payload); err != nil {
    52→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    56→	if repoRoot == "" {
    57→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    61→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    62→	defer os.Remove(lockPath)
    63→
    64→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
    65→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
    66→
    67→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
    68→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
    74→		return nil // No changes to review
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Build file list for the prompt
    78→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    79→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
    80→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
    81→	}
    82→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
    83→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
    84→	}
    85→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
    86→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
    87→	}
    88→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
    89→
    90→	// Build review prompt
    91→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, fileList, diff)
    92→
    93→	// Call Claude CLI for review
    94→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)
    95→	if err != nil {
    96→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Write REVIEW.md
   100→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   101→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   105→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   106→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Update dedup state
   110→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   111→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   112→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   113→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   114→
   115→	// Save state from repo root context
   116→	if err := os.Chdir(repoRoot); err == nil {
   117→		_ = saveWingmanState(&WingmanState{ //nolint:errcheck // best-effort state save
   118→			SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   119→			FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   120→			ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   121→			ReviewApplied: false,
   122→		})
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle
   126→	time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   127→
   128→	if !isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID) {
   129→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue
   133→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	return nil
   138→}
   139→
   140→// computeDiff gets the code changes to review. baseCommit is the HEAD hash
   141→// captured at trigger time so the diff is stable even if HEAD moves during
   142→// the initial delay (e.g., auto-commit creates another commit, or user commits).
   143→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, baseCommit string) (string, error) {
   144→	// Use the captured base commit when available for deterministic diffs
   145→	diffRef := "HEAD"
   146→	if baseCommit != "" {
   147→		diffRef = baseCommit
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// Try uncommitted changes against the base commit
   151→
   152→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff", diffRef)
   153→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   154→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   155→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   156→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   157→
   158→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   159→		// If the ref doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged
   160→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff")
   161→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   162→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   163→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   164→		if err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {
   165→			return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)", err, stderr.String())
   166→		}
   167→		return stdout2.String(), nil
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	// If no uncommitted changes, the commit itself may contain the changes
   171→	// (auto-commit strategy creates commits on the active branch)
   172→	if strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == "" {
   173→
   174→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "diff", diffRef+"~1", diffRef)
   175→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   176→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   177→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   178→		if err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != "" {
   179→			return stdout2.String(), nil
   180→		}
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	return stdout.String(), nil
   184→}
   185→
   186→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   187→func callClaudeForReview(prompt string) (string, error) {
   188→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "claude", "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", wingmanReviewModel, "--setting-sources", "")
   189→
   190→	// Isolate from git repo to prevent hooks and index pollution
   191→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   192→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   193→
   194→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   195→
   196→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   197→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   198→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   199→
   200→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   201→		var execErr *exec.Error
   202→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   203→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   204→		}
   205→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   206→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   207→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   208→		}
   209→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Parse the CLI response
   213→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   214→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   215→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   219→}
   220→
   221→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   222→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   223→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   224→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   225→		return false
   226→	}
   227→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   228→}
   229→
   230→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   231→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   232→	applyPrompt := "Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion that you agree with. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md."
   233→
   234→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "claude", "--continue", "-p", applyPrompt, "--setting-sources", "")
   235→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   236→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, but keep other env
   237→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   238→
   239→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   240→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   241→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   242→
   243→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   244→}
   245→
   246→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   247→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   248→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   249→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   250→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   251→	for _, e := range env {
   252→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   253→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   254→		}
   255→	}
   256→	return filtered
   257→}
   258→

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"syscall"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    14→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    15→// after the parent exits.
    16→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadJSON string) {
    17→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    18→	if err != nil {
    19→		return
    20→	}
    21→
    22→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadJSON is controlled internally, not user input
    23→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadJSON)
    24→
    25→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    26→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    27→		Setpgid: true,
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	// Don't hold the working directory
    31→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    32→
    33→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    34→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    35→
    36→	// Discard stdout/stderr to prevent output leaking to parent's terminal
    37→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    38→	cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    39→
    40→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    41→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    42→		return
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    46→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    47→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    48→}
    49→

     1→package cli

    12→
    13→func TestComputeFilesHash_Deterministic(t *testing.T) {
    14→	t.Parallel()
    15→
    16→	files := []string{"b.go", "a.go", "c.go"}
    17→	hash1 := computeFilesHash(files)
    18→	hash2 := computeFilesHash(files)
    19→
    20→	if hash1 != hash2 {
    21→		t.Errorf("expected deterministic hash, got %s and %s", hash1, hash2)
    22→	}
    23→
    24→	// Order shouldn't matter
    25→	files2 := []string{"c.go", "a.go", "b.go"}
    26→	hash3 := computeFilesHash(files2)
    27→
    28→	if hash1 != hash3 {
    29→		t.Errorf("expected order-independent hash, got %s and %s", hash1, hash3)
    30→	}
    31→}
    32→
    33→func TestComputeFilesHash_DifferentFiles(t *testing.T) {
    34→	t.Parallel()
    35→
    36→	hash1 := computeFilesHash([]string{"a.go", "b.go"})
    37→	hash2 := computeFilesHash([]string{"a.go", "c.go"})
    38→
    39→	if hash1 == hash2 {
    40→		t.Error("expected different hashes for different file lists")
    41→	}
    42→}
    43→
    44→func TestComputeFilesHash_Empty(t *testing.T) {
    45→	t.Parallel()
    46→
    47→	hash := computeFilesHash(nil)
    48→	if hash == "" {
    49→		t.Error("expected non-empty hash for empty file list")
    50→	}
    51→}
    52→
    53→func TestWingmanPayload_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
    54→	t.Parallel()
    55→
    56→	payload := WingmanPayload{
    57→		SessionID:     "test-session-123",
    58→		RepoRoot:      "/tmp/repo",
    59→		BaseCommit:    "abc123def456",
    60→		ModifiedFiles: []string{"main.go", "util.go"},
    61→		NewFiles:      []string{"new.go"},
    62→		DeletedFiles:  []string{"old.go"},
    63→		Prompts:       []string{"Fix the bug", "Add tests"},
    64→		CommitMessage: "fix: resolve issue",
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	data, err := json.Marshal(payload)
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	var decoded WingmanPayload
    73→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &decoded); err != nil {
    74→		t.Fatalf("failed to unmarshal: %v", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if decoded.SessionID != payload.SessionID {

    94→	}

   103→func TestBuildReviewPrompt_IncludesAllSections(t *testing.T) {
   104→	t.Parallel()
   105→
   106→	prompts := []string{"Fix the authentication bug"}
   107→	fileList := "auth.go (modified), auth_test.go (new)"
   108→	diff := `diff --git a/auth.go b/auth.go
   109→--- a/auth.go
   110→+++ b/auth.go
   111→@@ -10,6 +10,8 @@ func Login(user, pass string) error {
   112→+    if user == "" {
   113→+        return errors.New("empty user")
   114→+    }
   115→`
   116→
   117→	result := buildReviewPrompt(prompts, fileList, diff)
   118→
   119→	if !strings.Contains(result, "Fix the authentication bug") {

   127→	}
   128→	if !strings.Contains(result, "senior code reviewer") {
   129→		t.Error("prompt should contain reviewer instruction")
   130→	}
   131→}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/wingman.go",
  "old_string": "\t// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against\n\t// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.\n\tpayload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)\n\n\t// Write lock file synchronously before spawning to prevent races\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: lock file is not secrets\n\t_ = os.WriteFile(lockPath, []byte(payload.SessionID), 0o644) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort lock\n\n\t// Marshal payload\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(string(payloadJSON))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}",
  "new_string": "\t// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against\n\t// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.\n\tpayload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)\n\n\t// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,\n\t// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with\n\t// many files or long prompts.\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpayloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"wingman-payload.json\")\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadPath)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}\n\n// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale\n// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).\nconst staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute\n\n// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.\n// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed\n// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).\nfunc acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {\n\t//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant\n\tf, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Lock exists — check if it's stale\n\t\tinfo, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)\n\t\tif statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\\n\")\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\\n\",\n\t\t\ttime.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath) //nolint:errcheck // will retry create\n\t\t// Retry the atomic create\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant\n\t\tf, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID)\n\t_ = f.Close()\n\treturn true\n}"
}

}

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me provide a comprehensive report:

## Key Findings: Logging Infrastructure for Wingman Background Review

### 1. **Logging API Overview**

The logging package (`cmd/entire/cli/logging/`) uses Go's standard `log/slog` library with JSON output. Key functions:

**Initialization & Lifecycle:**
- `logging.Init(sessionID string)` - Initializes logger for a session, writes JSON logs to `.entire/logs/entire.log`
- `logging.Close()` - Flushes buffered data and closes log file (safe to call multiple times)
- `logging.SetLogLevelGetter(getter func() string)` - Sets callback to get log level from settings

**Logging Functions:**
- `logging.Debug(ctx, msg, attrs...)`
- `logging.Info(ctx, msg, attrs...)`
- `logging.Warn(ctx, msg, attrs...)`
- `logging.Error(ctx, msg, attrs...)`
- `logging.LogDuration(ctx, level, msg, startTime, attrs...)` - Logs with `duration_ms` field

**Context Enrichment:**
```go
ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
ctx = logging.WithSession(ctx, sessionID)
ctx = logging.WithToolCall(ctx, toolCallID)
ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agentName)
ctx = logging.WithParentSession(ctx, parentSessionID)
```

### 2. **Log File Location & Configuration**

**Path:** `.entire/logs/entire.log` (relative to repo root)

**Log Level Control (in order of precedence):**
1. `ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL` environment variable (DEBUG, INFO, WARN, ERROR)
2. Settings callback (via `SetLogLevelGetter()`)
3. Default: INFO level

**Log Format:** JSON (one entry per line)

**Fallback Behavior:** If `.entire/logs/` cannot be created or log file cannot be opened, logs fall back to stderr without error.

**Key File Paths:**
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging/logger.go` - Main implementation
- `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging/context.go` - Context value extraction
- Constant: `LogsDir = ".entire/logs"` (line 42 in logger.go)

### 3. **How Existing Code Uses Logging**

**In hooks (hooks_claudecode_handlers.go):**
```go
// Create context with component and agent
logCtx := logging.WithAgent(
    logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"),
    ag.Name()
)

// Log with structured attributes
logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
)
```

**In strategy operations (manual_commit_condensation.go):**
```go
logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed", slog.Error("error", err))
logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped", ...)
```

**Duration logging pattern:**
```go
defer logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, "operation completed", time.Now(),
    slog.String("hook", hookName),
    slog.Bool("success", true),
)
```

**Pattern in commands (clean.go):**
```go
logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)  // Set callback to read from settings
if err := logging.Init(""); err == nil {
    defer logging.Close()
}
```

### 4. **Wingman Background Review: Current Architecture**

**Flow:**
1. `triggerWingmanReview(payload)` - Called from stop hook in hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:393
2. `spawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadPath)` - Unix-specific detached spawn (wingman_spawn_unix.go:17)
3. `runWingmanReview(payloadPath)` - Executed in background subprocess (wingman_review.go:58)

**Current stdout/stderr Handling (wingman_spawn_unix.go:37-39):**
```go
cmd.Stdout = io.Discard  // Stdout discarded
cmd.Stderr = io.Discard  // Stderr discarded
```

**Why detached process is used:**
- Process group detachment via `syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}` (line 28)
- Survives parent process (hook) exit
- Initial delay: 10 seconds before review starts (wingmanInitialDelay)

### 5. **Solution: Redirect Stderr to Log File Instead**

Instead of using `io.Discard`, you can redirect subprocess stderr to a log file:

**Approach:**
```go
// In wingman_spawn_unix.go
logPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire/logs/wingman-review.log")

// Create/append to log file
logFile, err := os.OpenFile(logPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
if err != nil {
    // Fallback to discard on error
    cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
} else {
    cmd.Stderr = logFile
    // Note: cannot defer Close() since process is detached
}
cmd.Stdout = io.Discard  // Keep stdout discarded
```

**Limitation:** Cannot use defer to close logFile because the process is detached and runs independently. The file remains open in the child process. This is acceptable for append-only log files.

**Alternative: Initialize logging in subprocess:**
In `runWingmanReview()`, you can initialize the logging package:
```go
logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)
if err := logging.Init("wingman-" + payload.SessionID); err != nil {
    // Proceed anyway, logs go to stderr (which was discarded)
}
defer logging.Close()
```

### 6. **Log Rotation & Cleanup**

**Current State:** No automatic log rotation in the codebase.

**Observed Pattern:** Logs accumulate in `.entire/logs/entire.log` indefinitely. The `clean` command removes orphaned session data but doesn't touch logs.

**Recommended Approach for Wingman Logging:**
- Use separate log file: `.entire/logs/wingman-review.log`
- Could implement optional rotation/cleanup (not currently in scope)
- Simple append-only approach is safe and tracks all background reviews

### 7. **Getting a Logging Context in Wingman**

To add proper structured logging in the wingman background process:

**Step 1: In wingman_spawn_unix.go (parent process)**
```go
// Already have sessionID from payload
logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)
if err := logging.Init("wingman-trigger"); err != nil {
    // Fallback to stderr
}
defer logging.Close()

logging.Info(
    logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman"),
    "spawning background review",
    slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID),
    slog.Int("file_count", len(payload.ModifiedFiles)),
)
```

**Step 2: In wingman_review.go (child process)**
```go
// Initialize logging in the detached subprocess
logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)
if err := logging.Init("wingman-" + payload.SessionID); err != nil {
    // Logs to stderr (or file via redirection)
}
defer logging.Close()

// Use logging throughout
ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "wingman")
logging.Info(ctx, "review started", slog.String("session_id", payload.SessionID))

// Later in the process
logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, "diff computed", start,
    slog.Int("lines", len(diff)),
)
```

### 8. **Summary Table**

| Aspect | Details |
|--------|---------|
| **Log Location** | `.entire/logs/entire.log` (appended) |
| **Format** | JSON (one entry per line) |
| **Level Control** | `ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL` env var or settings callback |
| **Key Functions** | `Init()`, `Close()`, `Debug/Info/Warn/Error()`, `LogDuration()` |
| **Context APIs** | `WithComponent()`, `WithSession()`, `WithAgent()`, `WithToolCall()` |
| **Wingman Subprocess** | Currently discards stderr; can redirect to `.entire/logs/wingman-review.log` |
| **Detach Mechanism** | `syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}` on Unix |
| **No Rotation** | Logs accumulate indefinitely; no built-in cleanup |
| **Import Path** | `github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging` |

### 9. **Best Practices**

1. **Always defer `logging.Close()`** after `logging.Init()` to flush buffered output
2. **Use context enrichment** for filtering: `WithComponent()`, `WithAgent()`, `WithSession()`
3. **Use `LogDuration()`** with defer for timing operations
4. **Include operational metadata** only (IDs, counts, paths, durations)
5. **Never log user content** (prompts, file contents, commit messages)
6. **Fallback gracefully**: logging won't error if `.entire/logs/` can't be created; it falls back to stderr

**The problem:** The detached subprocess discards both stdout and stderr (`io.Discard`), so all those `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, ...)` calls in the review process are completely invisible. When it fails, you get zero feedback.

**Proposed approach:**

1. **Initialize the existing `logging` package** inside `runWingmanReview()` — structured JSON logs to `.entire/logs/entire.log` with `component=wingman`, logging each step: payload read, diff, Claude CLI call, REVIEW.md write, idle check, auto-apply.

2. **Redirect the detached process stderr** to `.entire/logs/wingman.log` instead of `io.Discard` — catches panics and any unexpected crashes that happen before logging initializes.

3. **Add logging in `triggerWingmanReview()`** (parent process) — the hook already has logging context, so we can log the trigger decision (skipped due to lock, dedup, pending review, etc.).

This gives you `tail -f .entire/logs/entire.log` to watch the background process in real time, and `.entire/logs/wingman.log` as a crash-level safety net.

    58→func runWingmanReview(payloadPath string) error {
    59→	// Read payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)
    60→	payloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read payload file: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→	// Clean up payload file after reading
    65→	_ = os.Remove(payloadPath)
    66→
    67→	var payload WingmanPayload
    68→	if err := json.Unmarshal(payloadJSON, &payload); err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal payload: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
    73→	if repoRoot == "" {
    74→		return errors.New("repo_root is required in payload")
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)
    78→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
    79→	defer os.Remove(lockPath)
    80→
    81→	// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle
    82→	time.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)
    83→
    84→	// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time
    85→	diff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to compute diff: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if strings.TrimSpace(diff) == "" {
    91→		return nil // No changes to review
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	// Build file list for the prompt
    95→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
    96→	for _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {
    97→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (modified)")
    98→	}
    99→	for _, f := range payload.NewFiles {
   100→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (new)")
   101→	}
   102→	for _, f := range payload.DeletedFiles {
   103→		allFiles = append(allFiles, f+" (deleted)")
   104→	}
   105→	fileList := strings.Join(allFiles, ", ")
   106→
   107→	// Build review prompt
   108→	prompt := buildReviewPrompt(payload.Prompts, fileList, diff)
   109→
   110→	// Call Claude CLI for review
   111→	reviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get review from Claude: %w", err)
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Write REVIEW.md
   117→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   118→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   119→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→	//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets
   122→	if err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {
   123→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write REVIEW.md: %w", err)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using
   127→	// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   128→	allFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   129→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   130→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)
   131→	allFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   132→
   133→	saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{
   134→		SessionID:     payload.SessionID,
   135→		FilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),
   136→		ReviewedAt:    time.Now(),
   137→		ReviewApplied: false,
   138→	})
   139→
   140→	// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle
   141→	time.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)
   142→
   143→	if !isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID) {
   144→		return nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue
   148→	if err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {
   149→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to trigger auto-apply: %w", err)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	return nil
   153→}
   154→
   155→// computeDiff gets the code changes to review. baseCommit is the HEAD hash
   156→// captured at trigger time so the diff is stable even if HEAD moves during
   157→// the initial delay (e.g., auto-commit creates another commit, or user commits).
   158→func computeDiff(repoRoot string, baseCommit string) (string, error) {
   159→	// Use the captured base commit when available for deterministic diffs
   160→	diffRef := "HEAD"
   161→	if baseCommit != "" {
   162→		diffRef = baseCommit
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Try uncommitted changes against the base commit
   166→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   167→	defer cancel()
   168→
   169→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "diff", diffRef)
   170→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   171→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   172→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   173→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   174→
   175→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   176→		// If the ref doesn't exist (initial commit), try diff of staged/unstaged
   177→		ctx2, cancel2 := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   178→		defer cancel2()
   179→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(ctx2, "git", "diff")
   180→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   181→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   182→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   183→		if err2 := cmd2.Run(); err2 != nil {
   184→			return "", fmt.Errorf("git diff failed: %w (stderr: %s)", err, stderr.String())
   185→		}
   186→		return stdout2.String(), nil
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// If no uncommitted changes, the commit itself may contain the changes
   190→	// (auto-commit strategy creates commits on the active branch)
   191→	if strings.TrimSpace(stdout.String()) == "" {
   192→		ctx2, cancel2 := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanGitTimeout)
   193→		defer cancel2()
   194→
   195→		cmd2 := exec.CommandContext(ctx2, "git", "diff", diffRef+"~1", diffRef)
   196→		cmd2.Dir = repoRoot
   197→		var stdout2 bytes.Buffer
   198→		cmd2.Stdout = &stdout2
   199→		if err := cmd2.Run(); err == nil && strings.TrimSpace(stdout2.String()) != "" {
   200→			return stdout2.String(), nil
   201→		}
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	return stdout.String(), nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// callClaudeForReview calls the claude CLI to perform the review.
   208→func callClaudeForReview(prompt string) (string, error) {
   209→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanReviewTimeout)
   210→	defer cancel()
   211→
   212→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", wingmanReviewModel, "--setting-sources", "")
   213→
   214→	// Isolate from git repo to prevent hooks and index pollution
   215→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   216→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   217→
   218→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   219→
   220→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   221→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   222→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   223→
   224→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   225→		var execErr *exec.Error
   226→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   227→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   228→		}
   229→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   230→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   231→			return "", fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   232→		}
   233→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Parse the CLI response
   237→	var cliResponse wingmanCLIResponse
   238→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   239→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	return cliResponse.Result, nil
   243→}
   244→
   245→// isSessionIdle checks if the given session is in the IDLE phase.
   246→func isSessionIdle(sessionID string) bool {
   247→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   248→	if err != nil || state == nil {
   249→		return false
   250→	}
   251→	return state.Phase == session.PhaseIdle
   252→}
   253→
   254→// triggerAutoApply spawns claude --continue to apply the review suggestions.
   255→func triggerAutoApply(repoRoot string) error {
   256→	applyPrompt := "Read .entire/REVIEW.md. Apply each suggestion that you agree with. When done, delete .entire/REVIEW.md."
   257→
   258→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), wingmanApplyTimeout)
   259→	defer cancel()
   260→
   261→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--continue", "-p", applyPrompt, "--setting-sources", "")
   262→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   263→	// Strip GIT_* env vars to prevent hook interference, but keep other env
   264→	cmd.Env = wingmanStripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   265→
   266→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   267→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   268→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   269→
   270→	return cmd.Run() //nolint:wrapcheck // caller wraps
   271→}
   272→
   273→// wingmanStripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   274→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo
   275→// when we want isolation (e.g., running claude --print for review).
   276→func wingmanStripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   277→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   278→	for _, e := range env {
   279→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   280→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   281→		}
   282→	}
   283→	return filtered
   284→}
   285→
   286→// saveWingmanStateDirect writes the wingman state file directly to a known path
   287→// under repoRoot, avoiding os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).
   288→func saveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot string, state *WingmanState) {
   289→	statePath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanStateFile)
   290→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(statePath), 0o750); err != nil {
   291→		return
   292→	}
   293→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return
   296→	}
   297→	//nolint:gosec,errcheck // G306: state file is config, not secrets; best-effort write
   298→	_ = os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644)
   299→}
   300→

     1→//go:build unix
     2→
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"io"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"os/exec"
    10→	"syscall"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.
    14→// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.
    15→// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues
    16→// after the parent exits.
    17→func spawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadPath string) {
    18→	executable, err := os.Executable()
    19→	if err != nil {
    20→		return
    21→	}
    22→
    23→	//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input
    24→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, "wingman", "__review", payloadPath)
    25→
    26→	// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit
    27→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{
    28→		Setpgid: true,
    29→	}
    30→
    31→	// Don't hold the working directory
    32→	cmd.Dir = "/"
    33→
    34→	// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)
    35→	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
    36→
    37→	// Discard stdout/stderr to prevent output leaking to parent's terminal
    38→	cmd.Stdout = io.Discard
    39→	cmd.Stderr = io.Discard
    40→
    41→	// Start the process (non-blocking)
    42→	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
    43→		return
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Release the process so it can run independently
    47→	//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless
    48→	_ = cmd.Process.Release()
    49→}
    50→
```

   167→					fmt.Fprintln(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Pending review: .entire/REVIEW.md")
   168→				}

   175→
   176→// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.
   177→func triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {
   178→	repoRoot := payload.RepoRoot
   179→
   180→	// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists
   181→	reviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)
   182→	if _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {
   183→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\n")
   184→		return
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL
   188→	// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.
   189→	lockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)
   190→	if !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {
   191→		return
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths
   195→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))
   196→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)
   197→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)
   198→	allFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)
   199→	filesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)
   200→
   201→	state, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup
   202→	if state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {
   203→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\n")
   204→		return
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against
   208→	// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.
   209→	payload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)
   210→
   211→	// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,
   212→	// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with
   213→	// many files or long prompts.
   214→	payloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\n", err)
   217→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   218→		return
   219→	}
   220→	payloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".entire", "wingman-payload.json")
   221→	//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets
   222→	if err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {
   223→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\n", err)
   224→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   225→		return
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file
   229→	spawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadPath)
   230→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review starting in background...\n")
   231→}
   232→
   233→// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
   234→// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
   235→const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
   236→
   237→// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
   238→// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
   239→// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
   240→func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
   241→	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   242→	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   243→	if err != nil {
   244→		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
   245→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
   246→			return false
   247→		}
   248→		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
   249→		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
   250→		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
   251→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
   252→			return false
   253→		}
   254→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
   255→			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
   256→		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
   257→		// Retry the atomic create
   258→		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
   259→		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   260→		if err != nil {
   261→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
   262→			return false
   263→		}
   264→	}
   265→	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
   266→	_ = f.Close()
   267→	return true
   268→}
   269→
   270→// resolveHEAD returns the current HEAD commit hash, or empty string on error.
   271→func resolveHEAD(repoRoot string) string {
   272→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   273→	cmd.Dir = repoRoot
   274→	out, err := cmd.Output()
   275→	if err != nil {
   276→		return ""
   277→	}
   278→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
   279→}
   280→
   281→// computeFilesHash returns a SHA256 hex digest of the sorted file paths.
   282→// Uses null byte separator (impossible in filenames) to avoid ambiguity.
   283→func computeFilesHash(files []string) string {
   284→	sorted := make([]string, len(files))
   285→	copy(sorted, files)
   286→	sort.Strings(sorted)
   287→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(sorted, "\x00")))
   288→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
   289→}
   290→
   291→// loadWingmanState loads the wingman state from .entire/wingman-state.json.
   292→func loadWingmanState() (*WingmanState, error) {
   293→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath) //nolint:gosec // path is repo-relative
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading wingman state: %w", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	var state WingmanState
   304→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   305→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing wingman state: %w", err)
   306→	}
   307→	return &state, nil
   308→}
   309→
   310→// saveWingmanState saves the wingman state to .entire/wingman-state.json.
   311→func saveWingmanState(state *WingmanState) error {
   312→	statePath, err := paths.AbsPath(wingmanStateFile)
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		return fmt.Errorf("resolving wingman state path: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	dir := filepath.Dir(statePath)
   318→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   319→		return fmt.Errorf("creating wingman state directory: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
   323→	if err != nil {
   324→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling wingman state: %w", err)
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	//nolint:gosec // G306: state file is config, not secrets
   328→	if err := os.WriteFile(statePath, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   329→		return fmt.Errorf("writing wingman state: %w", err)
   330→	}
   331→	return nil
   332→}
   333→

     1→// Package logging provides structured logging for the Entire CLI using slog.
     2→//
     3→// Usage:
     4→//
     5→//	// Initialize logger for a session (typically at session start)
     6→//	if err := logging.Init(sessionID); err != nil {
     7→//	    // handle error
     8→//	}
     9→//	defer logging.Close()
    10→//
    11→//	// Add context values
    12→//	ctx = logging.WithSession(ctx, sessionID)
    13→//	ctx = logging.WithToolCall(ctx, toolCallID)
    14→//
    15→//	// Log with context - session/tool IDs extracted automatically
    16→//	logging.Info(ctx, "hook invoked",
    17→//	    slog.String("hook", hookName),
    18→//	    slog.String("branch", branch),
    19→//	)
    20→package logging
    21→
    22→import (
    23→	"bufio"
    24→	"context"
    25→	"fmt"
    26→	"io"
    27→	"log/slog"
    28→	"os"
    29→	"path/filepath"
    30→	"strings"
    31→	"sync"
    32→	"time"
    33→
    34→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    35→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    36→)
    37→
    38→// LogLevelEnvVar is the environment variable that controls log level.
    39→const LogLevelEnvVar = "ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL"
    40→
    41→// LogsDir is the directory where log files are stored (relative to repo root).
    42→const LogsDir = ".entire/logs"
    43→
    44→var (
    45→	// logger is the package-level logger instance
    46→	logger *slog.Logger
    47→
    48→	// logFile holds the current log file handle for cleanup
    49→	logFile *os.File
    50→
    51→	// logBufWriter wraps logFile with buffered I/O for performance
    52→	logBufWriter *bufio.Writer
    53→
    54→	// currentSessionID stores the session ID from Init() to include in all logs
    55→	currentSessionID string
    56→
    57→	// mu protects logger, logFile, logBufWriter, and currentSessionID
    58→	mu sync.RWMutex
    59→
    60→	// logLevelGetter is an optional callback to get log level from settings.
    61→	// Set by SetLogLevelGetter before Init is called.
    62→	logLevelGetter func() string
    63→)
    64→
    65→// SetLogLevelGetter sets a callback function to get the log level from settings.
    66→// This allows the logging package to read settings without a circular dependency.
    67→// The callback is only used if ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL env var is not set.
    68→func SetLogLevelGetter(getter func() string) {
    69→	mu.Lock()
    70→	defer mu.Unlock()
    71→	logLevelGetter = getter
    72→}
    73→
    74→// Init initializes the logger for a session, writing JSON logs to
    75→// .entire/logs/entire.log.
    76→//
    77→// If sessionID is non-empty, it is stored as an slog attribute on every log line for filtering.
    78→// If the log file cannot be created, falls back to stderr.
    79→// Log level is controlled by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    80→func Init(sessionID string) error {
    81→	// Validate session ID if provided (used only for the slog attribute, not the filename)
    82→	if sessionID != "" {
    83→		if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
    84→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID for logging: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	mu.Lock()
    89→	defer mu.Unlock()
    90→
    91→	// Close any existing log file (flush buffer first)
    92→	if logBufWriter != nil {
    93→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
    94→		logBufWriter = nil
    95→	}
    96→	if logFile != nil {
    97→		_ = logFile.Close()
    98→		logFile = nil
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	// Get log level from environment first, then settings
   102→	levelStr := os.Getenv(LogLevelEnvVar)
   103→	if levelStr == "" && logLevelGetter != nil {
   104→		levelStr = logLevelGetter()
   105→	}
   106→	level := parseLogLevel(levelStr)
   107→
   108→	// Warn if invalid level was provided
   109→	if levelStr != "" && !isValidLogLevel(levelStr) {
   110→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: invalid log level %q, defaulting to INFO\n", levelStr)
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Determine log file path
   114→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		// Fall back to current directory
   117→		repoRoot = "."
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	logsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, LogsDir)
   121→	if err := os.MkdirAll(logsPath, 0o750); err != nil {
   122→		// Fall back to stderr
   123→		logger = createLogger(os.Stderr, level)
   124→		return nil
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	logFilePath := filepath.Join(logsPath, "entire.log")
   128→	f, err := os.OpenFile(logFilePath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600) //nolint:gosec // fixed filename, not user-controlled
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		// Fall back to stderr
   131→		logger = createLogger(os.Stderr, level)
   132→		return nil
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	logFile = f
   136→	logBufWriter = bufio.NewWriterSize(f, 8192) // 8KB buffer for batched writes
   137→	logger = createLogger(logBufWriter, level)
   138→	currentSessionID = sessionID
   139→
   140→	return nil
   141→}
   142→
   143→// Close closes the log file if one is open.
   144→// Flushes any buffered data before closing.
   145→// Safe to call multiple times.
   146→func Close() {
   147→	mu.Lock()
   148→	defer mu.Unlock()
   149→
   150→	if logBufWriter != nil {
   151→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
   152→		logBufWriter = nil
   153→	}
   154→	if logFile != nil {
   155→		_ = logFile.Close()
   156→		logFile = nil
   157→	}
   158→	currentSessionID = ""
   159→}
   160→
   161→// resetLogger resets the logger to nil (for testing).
   162→func resetLogger() {
   163→	mu.Lock()
   164→	defer mu.Unlock()
   165→	logger = nil
   166→	currentSessionID = ""
   167→	if logBufWriter != nil {
   168→		_ = logBufWriter.Flush()
   169→		logBufWriter = nil
   170→	}
   171→	if logFile != nil {
   172→		_ = logFile.Close()
   173→		logFile = nil
   174→	}
   175→}
   176→
   177→// getLogger returns the current logger, or a default stderr logger if not initialized.
   178→func getLogger() *slog.Logger {
   179→	mu.RLock()
   180→	defer mu.RUnlock()
   181→
   182→	if logger == nil {
   183→		// Return default stderr logger
   184→		return slog.Default()
   185→	}
   186→	return logger
   187→}
   188→
   189→// getSessionID returns the current session ID (thread-safe).
   190→func getSessionID() string {
   191→	mu.RLock()
   192→	defer mu.RUnlock()
   193→	return currentSessionID
   194→}
   195→
   196→// createLogger creates a JSON logger writing to the given writer at the specified level.
   197→func createLogger(w io.Writer, level slog.Level) *slog.Logger {
   198→	opts := &slog.HandlerOptions{
   199→		Level: level,
   200→	}
   201→	handler := slog.NewJSONHandler(w, opts)
   202→	return slog.New(handler)
   203→}
   204→
   205→// parseLogLevel parses a log level string to slog.Level.
   206→// Returns slog.LevelInfo for empty or invalid values.
   207→func parseLogLevel(s string) slog.Level {
   208→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
   209→	case "DEBUG":
   210→		return slog.LevelDebug
   211→	case "INFO":
   212→		return slog.LevelInfo
   213→	case "WARN", "WARNING":
   214→		return slog.LevelWarn
   215→	case "ERROR":
   216→		return slog.LevelError
   217→	default:
   218→		return slog.LevelInfo
   219→	}
   220→}
   221→
   222→// isValidLogLevel checks if the given string is a valid log level.
   223→func isValidLogLevel(s string) bool {
   224→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
   225→	case "DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "WARNING", "ERROR", "":
   226→		return true
   227→	default:
   228→		return false
   229→	}
   230→}
   231→
   232→// Debug logs at DEBUG level with context values automatically extracted.
   233→func Debug(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   234→	log(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, msg, attrs...)
   235→}
   236→
   237→// Info logs at INFO level with context values automatically extracted.
   238→func Info(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   239→	log(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, msg, attrs...)
   240→}
   241→
   242→// Warn logs at WARN level with context values automatically extracted.
   243→func Warn(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   244→	log(ctx, slog.LevelWarn, msg, attrs...)
   245→}
   246→
   247→// Error logs at ERROR level with context values automatically extracted.
   248→func Error(ctx context.Context, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   249→	log(ctx, slog.LevelError, msg, attrs...)
   250→}
   251→
   252→// LogDuration logs a message with duration_ms calculated from the start time.
   253→// The level parameter specifies the log level (use slog.LevelDebug, slog.LevelInfo, etc).
   254→// Designed for use with defer:
   255→//
   256→//	defer logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelInfo, "operation completed", time.Now())
   257→//
   258→// Or with additional attrs:
   259→//
   260→//	defer logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook executed", start,
   261→//	    slog.String("hook", hookName),
   262→//	    slog.Bool("success", true),
   263→//	)
   264→func LogDuration(ctx context.Context, level slog.Level, msg string, start time.Time, attrs ...any) {
   265→	durationMs := time.Since(start).Milliseconds()
   266→
   267→	// Prepend duration_ms to attrs
   268→	allAttrs := make([]any, 0, len(attrs)+1)
   269→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, slog.Int64("duration_ms", durationMs))
   270→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, attrs...)
   271→
   272→	log(ctx, level, msg, allAttrs...)
   273→}
   274→
   275→// log is the internal logging function that extracts context values and logs.
   276→func log(ctx context.Context, level slog.Level, msg string, attrs ...any) {
   277→	l := getLogger()
   278→
   279→	// Build attributes slice with session ID first (if set)
   280→	var allAttrs []any
   281→
   282→	// Add session ID from Init() if set (always first for consistency)
   283→	globalSessionID := getSessionID()
   284→	if globalSessionID != "" {
   285→		allAttrs = append(allAttrs, slog.String("session_id", globalSessionID))
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	// Extract context values, skipping session_id if already added from Init()
   289→	contextAttrs := attrsFromContext(ctx, globalSessionID)
   290→	for _, a := range contextAttrs {
   291→		allAttrs = append(allAttrs, a)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	// Add caller-provided attributes
   295→	allAttrs = append(allAttrs, attrs...)
   296→
   297→	// Pass nil context to slog as we've already extracted context values as attributes.
   298→	// slog handlers are expected to handle nil context gracefully.
   299→	l.Log(nil, level, msg, allAttrs...) //nolint:staticcheck // nil context is intentional - we extract values as attributes
   300→}
   301→
   302→// attrsFromContext extracts logging attributes from a context.
   303→// If globalSessionID is non-empty, skips adding session_id from context to avoid duplicates.
   304→func attrsFromContext(ctx context.Context, globalSessionID string) []slog.Attr {
   305→	if ctx == nil {
   306→		return nil
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var attrs []slog.Attr
   310→
   311→	// Only add session_id from context if not already set globally
   312→	if globalSessionID == "" {
   313→		if v := ctx.Value(sessionIDKey); v != nil {
   314→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   315→				attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("session_id", s))
   316→			}
   317→		}
   318→	}
   319→	if v := ctx.Value(parentSessionIDKey); v != nil {
   320→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   321→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("parent_session_id", s))
   322→		}
   323→	}
   324→	if v := ctx.Value(toolCallIDKey); v != nil {
   325→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   326→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("tool_call_id", s))
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→	if v := ctx.Value(componentKey); v != nil {
   330→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   331→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("component", s))
   332→		}
   333→	}
   334→	if v := ctx.Value(agentKey); v != nil {
   335→		if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   336→			attrs = append(attrs, slog.String("agent", s))
   337→		}
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	return attrs
   341→}
   342→

Good — `logging.Init()` relies on `paths.RepoRoot()` which uses cwd. The detached subprocess runs with `cmd.Dir = "/"`, so the logging package won't find the repo. I'll use a simpler approach for the subprocess: redirect stderr to a log file and write timestamped messages there.

Now let me implement all three changes.

  "new_string": "//go:build unix\n\npackage cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n)\n\n// spawnDetachedWingmanReview spawns a detached subprocess to run the wingman review.\n// repoRoot is the repository root used to locate the log file.\n// payloadPath is the path to the JSON payload file.\n// On Unix, this uses process group detachment so the subprocess continues\n// after the parent exits.\nfunc spawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath string) {\n\texecutable, err := os.Executable()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G204: payloadPath is controlled internally, not user input\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), executable, \"wingman\", \"__review\", payloadPath)\n\n\t// Detach from parent process group so subprocess survives parent exit\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{\n\t\tSetpgid: true,\n\t}\n\n\t// Don't hold the working directory\n\tcmd.Dir = \"/\"\n\n\t// Inherit environment (needed for PATH, git config, etc.)\n\tcmd.Env = os.Environ()\n\n\t// Redirect stderr to a log file for debugging the background process.\n\t// This catches panics, errors, and all wingmanLog() output.\n\tcmd.Stdout = io.Discard\n\tlogDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"logs\")\n\tif mkErr := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0o750); mkErr == nil {\n\t\t//nolint:gosec // G304: path is constructed from repoRoot + constants\n\t\tif f, openErr := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(logDir, \"wingman.log\"),\n\t\t\tos.O_CREATE|os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); openErr == nil {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = f\n\t\t\t// f stays open in the child process; the OS closes it on exit\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmd.Stderr = io.Discard\n\t}\n\n\t// Start the process (non-blocking)\n\tif err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Release the process so it can run independently\n\t//nolint:errcheck // Best effort - process should continue regardless\n\t_ = cmd.Process.Release()\n}"

O_CREATE|O_EXCL\n\t// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.\n\tlockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)\n\tif !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\tfilesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)\n\n\tstate, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup\n\tif state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against\n\t// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.\n\tpayload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)\n\n\t// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,\n\t// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with\n\t// many files or long prompts.\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpayloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"wingman-payload.json\")\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(payloadPath)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// triggerWingmanReview checks preconditions and spawns the detached review process.\nfunc triggerWingmanReview(payload WingmanPayload) {\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"wingman\")\n\trepoRoot := payload.RepoRoot\n\n\ttotalFiles := len(payload.ModifiedFiles) + len(payload.NewFiles) + len(payload.DeletedFiles)\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman trigger evaluating\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", payload.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"file_count\", totalFiles),\n\t)\n\n\t// Check if a pending REVIEW.md already exists\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(reviewPath); err == nil {\n\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman skipped: pending review exists\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Pending review exists, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Atomic lock file prevents concurrent review spawns. O_CREATE|O_EXCL\n\t// is atomic on all platforms, avoiding the TOCTOU race of Stat+WriteFile.\n\tlockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)\n\tif !acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, payload.SessionID) {\n\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman skipped: could not acquire lock\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Dedup check: compute hash of sorted file paths\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFiles = append(allFiles, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\tfilesHash := computeFilesHash(allFiles)\n\n\tstate, _ := loadWingmanState() //nolint:errcheck // best-effort dedup\n\tif state != nil && state.FilesHash == filesHash && state.SessionID == payload.SessionID {\n\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman skipped: dedup hash match\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"files_hash\", filesHash[:12]),\n\t\t)\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Already reviewed these changes, skipping\\n\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Capture HEAD at trigger time so the detached review diffs against\n\t// the correct commit even if HEAD moves during the initial delay.\n\tpayload.BaseCommit = resolveHEAD(repoRoot)\n\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"wingman captured base commit\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"base_commit\", payload.BaseCommit),\n\t)\n\n\t// Write payload to a temp file instead of passing as a CLI argument,\n\t// which can exceed OS argv limits (~128KB Linux, ~256KB macOS) with\n\t// many files or long prompts.\n\tpayloadJSON, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Error(logCtx, \"wingman failed to marshal payload\", slog.Any(\"error\", err))\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to marshal payload: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpayloadPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".entire\", \"wingman-payload.json\")\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: payload file is not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(payloadPath, payloadJSON, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Error(logCtx, \"wingman failed to write payload file\", slog.Any(\"error\", err))\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Failed to write payload file: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Spawn detached review process with path to payload file\n\tspawnDetachedWingmanReview(repoRoot, payloadPath)\n\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"wingman review spawned\",\n\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", payload.SessionID),\n\t\tslog.String(\"base_commit\", payload.BaseCommit),\n\t\tslog.Int(\"file_count\", totalFiles),\n\t)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[wingman] Review starting in background...\\n\")\n}"
}

  "old_string": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)",
  "new_string": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\n// wingmanLog writes a timestamped log line to stderr. In the detached subprocess,\n// stderr is redirected to .entire/logs/wingman.log by the spawner.\nfunc wingmanLog(format string, args ...any) {\n\tmsg := fmt.Sprintf(format, args...)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"%s [wingman] %s\\n\", time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339), msg)\n}"
}

payload from file (avoids OS argv limits with large payloads)\n\tpayloadJSON, err := os.ReadFile(payloadPath) //nolint:gosec // path is from our own spawn\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit, totalFiles)\n\n\t// Clean up lock file when review completes (regardless of success/failure)\n\tlockPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanLockFile)\n\tdefer func() {\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(lockPath)\n\t\twingmanLog(\"lock file removed\")\n\t}()\n\n\t// Initial delay: let the agent turn fully settle\n\twingmanLog(\"waiting %s for agent turn to settle\", wingmanInitialDelay)\n\ttime.Sleep(wingmanInitialDelay)\n\n\t// Compute diff using the base commit captured at trigger time\n\twingmanLog(\"computing diff against %s\", payload.BaseCommit)\n\tdiffStart := time.Now()\n\tdiff, err := computeDiff(repoRoot, payload.BaseCommit)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR computing diff: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to compute diff: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(diff) == \"\" {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"no changes found in diff, exiting\")\n\t\treturn nil // No changes to review\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"diff computed: %d bytes in %s\", len(diff), time.Since(diffStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\t// Build file list for the prompt\n\tallFiles := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tfor _, f := range payload.ModifiedFiles {\n\t\tallFiles = append(allFiles, f+\" (modified)\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, f := range
