Claude CLI for review\n\twingmanLog(\"calling claude CLI (model=%s, timeout=%s)\", wingmanReviewModel, wingmanReviewTimeout)\n\treviewStart := time.Now()\n\treviewText, err := callClaudeForReview(prompt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR claude CLI failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get review from Claude: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"review received: %d bytes in %s\", len(reviewText), time.Since(reviewStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\t// Write REVIEW.md\n\treviewPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, wingmanReviewFile)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(reviewPath), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR creating directory: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: review file is not secrets\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(reviewPath, []byte(reviewText), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR writing REVIEW.md: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write REVIEW.md: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"REVIEW.md written to %s\", reviewPath)\n\n\t// Update dedup state — write directly to known path instead of using\n\t// os.Chdir (which mutates process-wide state).\n\tallFilePaths := make([]string, 0, len(payload.ModifiedFiles)+len(payload.NewFiles)+len(payload.DeletedFiles))\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.ModifiedFiles...)\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.NewFiles...)\n\tallFilePaths = append(allFilePaths, payload.DeletedFiles...)\n\n\tsaveWingmanStateDirect(repoRoot, &WingmanState{\n\t\tSessionID:     payload.SessionID,\n\t\tFilesHash:     computeFilesHash(allFilePaths),\n\t\tReviewedAt:    time.Now(),\n\t\tReviewApplied: false,\n\t})\n\twingmanLog(\"dedup state saved\")\n\n\t// Auto-apply phase: wait then check if session is idle\n\twingmanLog(\"waiting %s before auto-apply check\", wingmanApplyDelay)\n\ttime.Sleep(wingmanApplyDelay)\n\n\tidle := isSessionIdle(payload.SessionID)\n\twingmanLog(\"session idle check: idle=%v\", idle)\n\n\tif !idle {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"session is active, leaving REVIEW.md for notification\")\n\t\treturn nil // User is active, leave REVIEW.md for notification\n\t}\n\n\t// Trigger auto-apply via claude --continue\n\twingmanLog(\"triggering auto-apply via claude --continue\")\n\tapplyStart := time.Now()\n\tif err := triggerAutoApply(repoRoot); err != nil {\n\t\twingmanLog(\"ERROR auto-apply failed after %s: %v\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond), err)\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to trigger auto-apply: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\twingmanLog(\"auto-apply completed in %s\", time.Since(applyStart).Round(time.Millisecond))\n\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was added:

**`.entire/logs/wingman.log`** — Detached process stderr redirect
- The spawner now redirects stderr to this file instead of `io.Discard`
- Catches panics, crashes, and all `wingmanLog()` output from the background process

**`wingman_review.go`** — Timestamped step-by-step logging via `wingmanLog()`
- Process start (pid)
- Payload read + session/repo/commit/file-count summary
- Initial delay (10s wait)
- Diff computation (bytes, duration)
- Prompt build (bytes)
- Claude CLI call (model, start, result bytes, duration)
- REVIEW.md write
- Dedup state save
- Auto-apply delay (30s wait)
- Session idle check result
- Auto-apply trigger (start, duration)
- All errors with context

**`wingman.go`** — Structured JSON logging via the `logging` package
- Trigger evaluation (session ID, file count)
- Skip reasons (pending review, lock held, dedup match)
- Base commit capture
- Spawn confirmation
- Errors (payload marshal, file write)

To debug: `tail -f .entire/logs/wingman.log` for the background process, or check `.entire/logs/entire.log` for trigger-side events.

+// staleLockThreshold is how old a lock file can be before we consider it stale
+// (e.g., the detached process was SIGKILLed and the defer never ran).
+const staleLockThreshold = 30 * time.Minute
+
+// acquireWingmanLock atomically creates the lock file. Returns true if acquired.
+// If the lock already exists but is older than staleLockThreshold, it is removed
+// and re-acquired (handles crashed detached processes).
+func acquireWingmanLock(lockPath, sessionID string) bool {
+	//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
+	f, err := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
+	if err != nil {
+		if !errors.Is(err, os.ErrExist) {
+			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock: %v\n", err)
+			return false
+		}
+		// Lock exists — check if it's stale
+		info, statErr := os.Stat(lockPath)
+		if statErr != nil || time.Since(info.ModTime()) <= staleLockThreshold {
+			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Review already in progress, skipping\n")
+			return false
+		}
+		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Removing stale lock (age: %s)\n",
+			time.Since(info.ModTime()).Round(time.Second))
+		_ = os.Remove(lockPath)
+		// Retry the atomic create
+		//nolint:gosec // G304: lockPath is constructed from repoRoot + constant
+		f, err = os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREATE|os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
+		if err != nil {
+			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[wingman] Failed to create lock after stale removal: %v\n", err)
+			return false
+		}
+	}
+	_, _ = f.WriteString(sessionID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort session ID write
+	_ = f.Close()
+	return true
+}
